MODEL     : z-ai/glm-5.3-flash
METRIC    : area_delay_product
RESULT    : PASS  |  best cost: 4.163e+06 area_delay_product  (eval 12)

[SYSTEM]
You are an RTL (Register Transfer Level) design agent. Your task is to create a Verilog/SystemVerilog design that satisfies the given specification, is functionally correct, and has minimal cost (area_delay_product).

**Cost metric `area_delay_product`:** area × achieved critical-path delay — the classic area-delay product. Does NOT include simulation cycle count (contrast with the 'area_runtime_product' metric). Cost is measured on your design alone. The testbench and everything it owns (checking logic, the external memories, etc.) is not synthesized and contributes nothing to area, delay, power, or energy.

## Specification
Full 8x8 2-D IDCT of a baseline JPEG decoder (ultraembedded/core_jpeg), module
name `jpeg_idct_2d`: the row pass (jpeg_idct_x, OUT_SHIFT=11), a double-buffered
corner-turn (jpeg_idct_transpose with four 32x32 dual-port RAMs), and the column
pass (jpeg_idct_y, OUT_SHIFT=15), composed exactly as in the original decoder
(downstream ready tied high; the row pass accepts no backpressure). Each 1-D
pass is the factorized (AAN-style) algorithm with cosine constants scaled by
4096 (C1..C7 = 4017, 3784, 3406, 2896, 2276, 1567, 799), time-sharing 5
multipliers over an 8-beat schedule. ALL arithmetic is evaluated in 32-bit
signed context with wrap-around; outputs of each pass are arithmetic-shifted
right (11 then 15); the 1/sqrt(2) odd-part scaling is `(x * 181) / 256` where
`*` wraps at 32 bits and `/` is SIGNED division truncating toward zero. This
exact fixed-point behavior is what the golden vectors encode.

Interface (clocked, sync active-high reset `rst`; no backpressure):
  inputs : clk, rst, img_start_i (1-cycle pulse resets the output streamers),
           img_end_i (unused), inport_valid_i, inport_idx_i[2:0],
           inport_data0..3_i[15:0]
  outputs: outport_valid_o, outport_data_o[31:0], outport_idx_o[5:0]

Input protocol (the upstream block serializer's FIXED schedule, reproduced
exactly by the testbench): per row of 8 coefficients c0..c7 (16-bit signed),
8 back-to-back valid beats with idx = 0..7; the four data lanes carry the EVEN
set (c0,c2,c4,c6) on beats idx in {0,2,5,7} and the ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7) on
beats idx in {1,3,4,6}. 8 rows back-to-back form a 64-beat block; between
blocks there are >= 2 idle cycles (valid=0, idx=0, data=0). Pipeline registers
advance every clock regardless of valid — designs must be robust to the gap
pattern exactly as the original is.

Output (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on outport_valid_o; the
k-th streamed value of a block carries outport_idx_o = {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the
column pass emits column-major; the idx label gives the ROW-MAJOR pixel
position) and must match the expected output at that position. The stream of a
block overlaps the next block's input beats (the transpose double-buffers one
full block).

A working starting point is provided: `starting_point.v` contains the
ORIGINAL core_jpeg modules (Apache 2.0), unmodified, under a thin composition
top. It passes all golden vectors (they were generated from it — the original
RTL is the correctness oracle). Study it, then optimize while preserving its
exact behavior. Sibling benchmark: `jpeg_idct_2d_spire` (identical tb.sv +
vectors.dat, bit-exact SpireHDL starting point). The 1-D row pass alone is
also available as the `jpeg_idct_x_*` benchmarks.

NOTE on memories: the cost-evaluation synthesis flow targets a standard-cell
library with no RAM macros, so every behavioral memory in this design (the
transpose buffers, block_out, etc.) is inferred as a register array plus mux
decode REGARDLESS of coding style. Optimizing that decode/mux logic is fair
game; instantiating a hard RAM macro is not possible in this flow.

## Tools
- create_file: Create a new file in your working directory
- replace_file: Replace the entire contents of an existing file
- apply_diff: Apply a unified diff to modify a file
- edit_file: Edit a file by replacing an exact string match with new content. Provide old_string (must match exactly once) and new_string.
- ls: List files in your working directory
- read_file: Read the contents of a file
- run_evaluation(filename[, target_delay]): Run evaluation on the given design file. Returns correctness (Verilator simulation) + cost (Yosys area_delay_product). The filename must be your main design file (e.g. 'design.sv'). Optionally pass target_delay (ps) to override the synthesis timing constraint.

## Strategy
1. Lay out an action plan. Try to cover a diverse set of approaches in your plan to increase the chances of finding a good solution within the step limit. Also once you find a new best solution, explore close solutions. Trade off exploration with exploitation.
2. First, create a simple, straightforward design that is functionally correct. Focus on getting 100% correctness first.
3. Run evaluation to confirm correctness and get the initial cost (area_delay_product).
4. ONLY after achieving 100% correctness, try to optimize the design to reduce the area_delay_product.
5. After each optimization, run evaluation to verify correctness is maintained and check the new cost.
6. If an optimization breaks correctness, revert and try a different approach.
7. Keep iterating until you run out of steps — use every step to try improvements. The run ends automatically at the step limit; your best passing design is kept.

In general, be creative and take risk. At least after you have a correct design, try bold optimizations to reduce area_delay_product, even if they might break correctness. You can always revert if it doesn't work.
The best results you obtain which is 100% correctness counts.
Try to beat at least the straightforward implementation.

Running evaluation is crucial after every change to ensure correctness and track cost. Do not skip evaluations.

You may do some 'thinking' alongside with your tool calling responses to explain your reasoning, but always remember to call a tool in every response.

## Important
- Always call a tool in every response.
- You have a maximum of **60 steps** (LLM turns). Budget them wisely: get a correct design first, then optimise. You may batch tool calls in one response --- at most 8, and only one `run_evaluation`.
- The best result is the one with lowest cost among designs that pass 100% of testbench checks.
- Use different filenames for different design variants so you can retrieve them later.
- If context files (e.g. component source files) are in your workspace, you can modify them directly using edit_file or replace_file to optimize the design. Make targeted, incremental changes rather than rewriting files from scratch — small edits are less likely to introduce bugs.
- Write clean, synthesizable RTL code.
- You can pass `target_delay` (ps) to `run_evaluation` to try different synthesis timing constraints; experimenting with this may improve PPA results (lower target delay may reduce delay, higher target delay may reduce area/transistor count). target_delay must be > 0.
- Do not spend too many steps sweeping `target_delay` — basically if you want to minimize delay choose a low value,if you want to minimize area/transistor count choose a high value. That's it.A few targeted experiments are more productive than an exhaustive sweep.

## Delay Optimization Tips
- `target_delay` trades area against delay (a lower value pressures toward faster logic, a higher value lets Yosys re-map to smaller cells). Do not overly iterate on different target delays; often the RTL structure is the real lever, target_delay is only a minor secondary knob.
- After getting a correct design, try different accumulation orderings (sequential vs. tree) and compare results — Yosys synthesis can produce noticeably different delays (~10 ps) from structurally similar descriptions.
- For designs dominated by a multiply-accumulate chain, the critical path is the multiplier followed by the adder tree. Reducing the adder depth or splitting into parallel partial sums can help.

## Reference Implementations
The following are reference implementations for inspiration. Study them for patterns and techniques, but do not copy them directly — adapt ideas to your specific design.

### Parallel-prefix adder (ripple-carry / carry-lookahead) (Add.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Binary adder using ripple-carry or parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.
// S = A+B

module Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);

	// Function: Binary adder using parallel-prefix carry-lookahead logic.

	logic [width-1:0] GI, PI;  // prefix gen./prop. in
	logic [width-1:0] GO, PO;  // prefix gen./prop. out
	logic [width-1:0] PT;  // adder propagate temp

	// Internal signals for unsigned operands
	logic [width-1:0] Auns, Buns, Suns;

	// default ripple-carry adder as slow implementation
	if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin
		// type conversion: std_logic_vector -> unsigned
		assign Auns = A;
		assign Buns = B;

		// addition
		assign Suns = Auns + Buns;

		// type conversion: unsigned -> std_logic_vector
		assign S = Suns;
	end else begin
		// parallel-prefix adders as medium and fast implementations

		// calculate prefix input generate/propagate signals
		assign GI = A & B;
		assign PI = A | B;
		// calculate adder propagate signals (PT = A xor B)
		assign PT = ~GI & PI;

		// calculate prefix output generate/propagate signals
		PrefixAndOr #(
			.width(width),
			.speed(speed)
		) prefix (
			.GI(GI),
			.PI(PI),
			.GO(GO),
			.PO(PO)
		);

		// calculate sum bits
		assign S = PT ^ {GO[width-2:0], 1'b0};
	end
endmodule



module behavioural_Add #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] A,  // operands
	input  logic [width-1:0] B,
	output logic [width-1:0] S   // sum
);
	assign S = A + B;
endmodule
```

### Prefix AND-OR structures (Sklansky, Brent-Kung, serial) (PrefixAndOr.sv)
```systemverilog
// Copyright 2024 ETH Zurich and University of Bologna.
// Solderpad Hardware License, Version 0.51, see LICENSE for details.
// SPDX-License-Identifier: SHL-0.51
//
// Based on the work by Reto Zimmermann 1998 - ETH Zürich
// Originally written in VHDL, available under: 
// https://iis-people.ee.ethz.ch/~zimmi/arith_lib.html#library
//
// Authors:
// - Thomas Benz <tbenz@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Philippe Sauter <phsauter@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
// - Paul Scheffler <paulsc@iis.ee.ethz.ch>
//
// Description :
// Prefix structures of different depth (i.e. speed) for carry calculation
// in binary adders. Compute in m levels new generate/propagate signal pairs
// for always larger groups of bits. Generate signals of the last level correspond
// to carries in binary addition. Basic logic operations: AND-OR for generate
// signals, AND for propagate signals.

module PrefixAndOr #(
	parameter int              width = 8,             // word width
	parameter lau_pkg::speed_e speed = lau_pkg::FAST  // performance parameter
) (
	input  logic [width-1:0] GI,  // gen./prop. in
	input  logic [width-1:0] PI,  // gen./prop. in
	output logic [width-1:0] GO,  // gen./prop. out
	output logic [width-1:0] PO   // gen./prop. out
);

	// Constants
	localparam int n = width;  // prefix structure width
	localparam int m = $clog2(width);  // prefix structure depth

	// Sklansky parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::FAST) begin : fastPrefix
		logic [(m+1)*n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
			assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
			assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;
			for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels
				for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** (m - l); k++) begin : groups
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2 ** (l - 1); i++) begin : bits
						// pass prop and gen to following nodes
						if ((k * 2 ** l + i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = GT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
							assign PT[l*n+k*2**l+i] = PT[(l-1)*n+k*2**l+i];
						end
						// calculate new propagate and generate
						if ((k * 2 ** l + 2 ** (l - 1) + i) < n) begin : black
							assign GT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
												GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
											  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												 & GT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1] );
							assign PT[l*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i] = 
													PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) + i]
												  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) - 1];
						end
					end
				end
			end
			assign GO = GT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
			assign PO = PT[(m+1)*n-1 : m*n];
	end

	// Brent-Kung parallel-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	if (speed == lau_pkg::MEDIUM) begin : mediumPrefix
		logic [(2*m)*n -1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[n-1:0] = GI;
		assign PT[n-1:0] = PI;

		for (genvar l = 1; l <= m; l++) begin : levels1
			for (genvar k = 0; k < 2**(m-l); k++) begin : groups
				for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**l -1; i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**l +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l +i];
					end // white
				end // bits
				if ((k* 2**l + 2**l -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l - 1] |
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							     & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**l + 2**(l-1) -1]);
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**l + 2**l -1] =
								PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**l     -1] 
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k*2**l + 2**(l-1) -1];
				end // black
			end
		end // level1
		for (genvar l = m +1; l < 2*m; l++) begin : levels2
			for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
				if (i < n) begin : white
					assign GT[l*n +i] = GT[(l-1)*n +i];
					assign PT[l*n +i] = PT[(l-1)*n +i];
				end // white
			end // bits
			for (genvar k = 1; k < 2**(l-m); k++) begin : groups
				if (l < 2*m -1) begin : empty
					for (genvar i = 0; i < 2**(2*m -l -1) -1; i++) begin : bits
						if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
							assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
							assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						end // white
					end
				end // empty
				if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l -1) -1) < n) begin : black
					assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  | (  PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) + 2**(2*m-l-1) -1] 
								 & GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m-l) -1] );
					assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1] = 
								PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) + 2**(2*m -l-1) -1]
							  & PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) -1];
				end // black
				for (genvar i = 2**(2*m -l-1); i < 2**(2*m -l); i++) begin : bits
					if ((k* 2**(2*m -l) +i) < n) begin : white
						assign GT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = GT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
						assign PT[l*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i] = PT[(l-1)*n + k* 2**(2*m -l) +i];
					end // white
				end
			end
		end // level2
		assign GO = GT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
		assign PO = PT[2*m*n -1 : (2*m -1) * n];
	end  // Serial-prefix carry-lookahead structure
	else if (speed == lau_pkg::SLOW) begin : slowPrefix
		logic [n-1:0] GT, PT;  // gen./prop. temp
		assign GT[0] = GI[0];
		assign PT[0] = PI[0];
		
		for (genvar i = 1; i < n; i++) begin : bits
			assign GT[i] = GI[i] | (PI[i] & GT[i-1]);
			assign PT[i] = PI[i] & PT[i-1];
		end
		assign GO = GT;
		assign PO = PT;
	end

endmodule

```

wire signed [31:0] block_in_2_3 = {{16{inport_data1_i[15]}}, inport_data1_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_4_5 = {{16{inport_data2_i[15]}}, inport_data2_i};
wire signed [31:0] block_in_6_7 = {{16{inport_data3_i[15]}}, inport_data3_i};

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// IDCT
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg signed [31:0] i0;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul0_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_b;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_a;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_b;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    i0     <= 32'b0;
    mul0_a <= 32'b0;
    mul0_b <= 32'b0;
    mul1_a <= 32'b0;
    mul1_b <= 32'b0;
    mul2_a <= 32'b0;
    mul2_b <= 32'b0;
    mul3_a <= 32'b0;
    mul3_b <= 32'b0;
    mul4_a <= 32'b0;
    mul4_b <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */
    case (inport_idx_i)
    3'd0:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C2_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C6_16;
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C1_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C7_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C5_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C3_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1;
        mul0_b <= C7_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C1_16;
        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5;
        mul2_b <= C3_16;
        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul3_b <= C5_16;
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3;
        mul0_b <= C6_16;
        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7;
        mul1_b <= C2_16;
        mul4_a <= i0;
        mul4_b <= C4_16;
    end
    default:
        ;
    endcase
    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul1_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul2_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul3_q;
reg signed [31:0] mul4_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0_q <= 32'b0;
    mul1_q <= 32'b0;
    mul2_q <= 32'b0;
    mul3_q <= 32'b0;
    mul4_q <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0_q <= mul0_a * mul0_b;
    mul1_q <= mul1_a * mul1_b;
    mul2_q <= mul2_a * mul2_b;
    mul3_q <= mul3_a * mul3_b;
    mul4_q <= mul4_a * mul4_b;
end

reg signed [31:0] mul0;
reg signed [31:0] mul1;
reg signed [31:0] mul2;
reg signed [31:0] mul3;
reg signed [31:0] mul4;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    mul0 <= 32'b0;
    mul1 <= 32'b0;
    mul2 <= 32'b0;
    mul3 <= 32'b0;
    mul4 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    mul0 <= mul0_q;
    mul1 <= mul1_q;
    mul2 <= mul2_q;
    mul3 <= mul3_q;
    mul4 <= mul4_q;
end

reg        out_stg0_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i;
    out_stg0_idx_q   <= inport_idx_i;
end

reg        out_stg1_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q;
    out_stg1_idx_q   <= out_stg0_idx_q;
end

reg        out_stg2_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q;
    out_stg2_idx_q   <= out_stg1_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] o_s5;
reg signed [31:0] o_s6;
reg signed [31:0] o_s7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t0;
reg signed [31:0] o_t1;
reg signed [31:0] o_t2;
reg signed [31:0] o_t3;
reg signed [31:0] o_t4;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6;
reg signed [31:0] o_t7;
reg signed [31:0] o_t6_5;
reg signed [31:0] o_t5_6;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    o_s5   <= 32'b0;
    o_s6   <= 32'b0;
    o_s7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t0   <= 32'b0;
    o_t1   <= 32'b0;
    o_t2   <= 32'b0;
    o_t3   <= 32'b0;
    o_t4   <= 32'b0;
    o_t5   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6   <= 32'b0;
    o_t7   <= 32'b0;
    o_t6_5 <= 32'b0;
    o_t5_6 <= 32'b0;
end
else
begin
    case (out_stg2_idx_q)
    3'd0:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; // s3
    end
    3'd1:
    begin
        o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1;
        o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3;
        o_t0 <= mul4;        // s0
    end
    3'd2:
    begin
        o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; // t0
        o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; // t3
        o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7;
    end
    3'd3:
    begin
        o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3);
    end
    3'd4:
    begin
        o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; // s4
        o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3;    
    end
    3'd5:
    begin
        o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; // s2
        o_t4 <= mul4; // s1
        o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5;
        o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6;
    end
    3'd6:
    begin
        o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3;
        o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3;
        o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5;
        o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6;
    end
    default:
    begin
        o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
        o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256; // 1/sqrt(2)
    end
    endcase
end

reg        out_stg3_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q;
    out_stg3_idx_q   <= out_stg2_idx_q;
end

reg signed [31:0] block_out[0:7];
reg signed [31:0] block_out_tmp;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_out[0] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[1] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[2] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[3] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[4] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[5] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[6] <= 32'b0;
    block_out[7] <= 32'b0;
    block_out_tmp <= 32'b0;
end
else if (out_stg3_valid_q)
begin
    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)
    begin
        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT); // block_out[7]
        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)
        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)
    begin
        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end
end

reg [7:0] valid_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    valid_q  <= 8'b0;
else
    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};

reg [5:0] ptr_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (img_start_i)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (outport_valid_o)
    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;

assign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];
assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];

assign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;



endmodule

[Step 3/60]

module jpeg_idct_transpose
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data_i
    ,input  [  5:0]  inport_idx_i
    ,input           outport_ready_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          inport_accept_o
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data1_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data2_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data3_o
    ,output [  2:0]  outport_idx_o
);



reg         block_wr_q;
reg         block_rd_q;
reg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;
reg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;

wire [4:0]  wr_ptr_w = {block_wr_q, inport_idx_i[3:0]};

wire [31:0] outport_data0_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data1_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data2_w;
wire [31:0] outport_data3_w;

wire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);
wire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);
wire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);
wire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram0
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr0_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data0_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram1
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr1_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data1_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram2
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr2_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data2_w)
);

jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
u_ram3
(
     .clk0_i(clk)
    ,.rst0_i(rst)
    ,.clk1_i(clk)
    ,.rst1_i(rst)

    ,.addr0_i(wr_ptr_w)
    ,.data0_i(inport_data_i)
    ,.wr0_i(wr3_w)
    ,.data0_o()

    ,.addr1_i({block_rd_q, rd_addr_q})
    ,.data1_i(32'b0)
    ,.wr1_i(1'b0)
    ,.data1_o(outport_data3_w)
);

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Input Buffer
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
reg [1:0] block_ready_q;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else if (img_start_i)
begin
    block_ready_q     <= 2'b0;
    block_wr_q        <= 1'b0;
    block_rd_q        <= 1'b0;
end
else
begin
    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;
        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;
    end

    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)
    begin
        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;
        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;
    end
end

assign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];

//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// FSM
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
localparam STATE_W           = 2;
localparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;
localparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;
localparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;

reg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;
reg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;

always @ *
begin
    next_state_r = state_q;

    case (state_q)
    STATE_IDLE:
    begin
        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)
            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;
    end
    STATE_SETUP:
    begin
        next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;
    end
    STATE_ACTIVE:
    begin
        if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63)
            next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
    end
    default: ;
    endcase

    if (img_start_i)
        next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;
end

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    state_q <= STATE_IDLE;
else
    state_q <= next_state_r;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
    rd_idx_q <= rd_idx_q + 6'd1;

always @ (posedge clk )
if (rst)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_IDLE)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;
else if (state_q == STATE_SETUP)
    rd_addr_q <= 4'd8;
else if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)
begin
    case (rd_idx_q[2:0])
    3'd0: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd1: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd2: ;
    3'd3: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd4: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    3'd5: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q - 4'd8;
    3'd6: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd1;
    3'd7: rd_addr_q <= rd_addr_q + 4'd8;
    endcase
end

assign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);
assign outport_idx_o   = rd_idx_q[2:0];
assign outport_data0_o = outport_data0_w;
assign outport_data1_o = outport_data1_w;
assign outport_data2_o = outport_data2_w;
assign outport_data3_o = outport_data3_w;


endmodule

module jpeg_idct_transpose_ram
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk0_i
    ,input           rst0_i
    ,input  [  4:0]  addr0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  data0_i
    ,input           wr0_i
    ,input           clk1_i
    ,input           rst1_i
    ,input  [  4:0]  addr1_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  data1_i
    ,input           wr1_i

    // Outputs
    ,output [ 31:0]  data0_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  data1_o
);



//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Dual Port RAM
// Mode: Read First
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
/* verilator lint_off MULTIDRIVEN */
reg [31:0]   ram [31:0] /*verilator public*/;
/* verilator lint_on MULTIDRIVEN */

reg [31:0] ram_read0_q;
reg [31:0] ram_read1_q;


// Synchronous write
always @ (posedge clk0_i)
begin
    if (wr0_i)
        ram[addr0_i][31:0] <= data0_i[31:0];

    ram_read0_q <= ram[addr0_i];
end

always @ (posedge clk1_i)
begin
    if (wr1_i)
        ram[addr1_i][31:0] <= data1_i[31:0];

    ram_read1_q <= ram[addr1_i];
end


assign data0_o = ram_read0_q;
assign data1_o = ram_read1_q;



endmodule

module jpeg_idct_y
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Params
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
#(
     parameter OUT_SHIFT        = 15
    ,parameter INPUT_WIDTH      = 32
)
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
// Ports
//-----------------------------------------------------------------
(
    // Inputs
     input           clk
    ,input           rst
    ,input           img_start_i
    ,input           img_end_i
    ,input           inport_valid_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data0_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data1_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data2_i
    ,input  [ 31:0]  inport_data3_i
    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i

    // Outputs
    ,output          outport_valid_o
    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o
    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o
);




localparam [15:0] C1_16 = 4017; // cos( pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C2_16 = 3784; // cos(2pi/16) x4096
localparam [15:0] C3_16 = 3406; // cos(3pi/16) x4096

// Self-checking testbench for jpeg_idct_2d — the full 8x8 2-D IDCT
// (x-pass -> transpose -> y-pass) of ultraembedded/core_jpeg.
// Reads vectors.dat: one line per 8x8 block = 64 input coefficients (16-bit
// signed, 4 hex digits, row-major c[row][col]) followed by 64 expected outputs
// (32-bit, 8 hex digits, in ROW-MAJOR pixel order — outputs are matched via
// outport_idx_o, which labels each streamed value with its row-major position).
//
// Drive protocol: identical to the jpeg_idct_x benchmark (the upstream block
// serializer's fixed schedule): per row 8 back-to-back valid beats idx=0..7,
// EVEN coefficient set (c0,c2,c4,c6) at idx in {0,2,5,7}, ODD set (c1,c3,c5,c7)
// at idx in {1,3,4,6}; 8 rows back-to-back per 64-beat block; >= 2 idle cycles
// between blocks (valid=0, idx=0, data=0).
//
// Output contract (fixed schedule): 64 values per block streamed on
// outport_valid_o; the k-th value of a block carries
// outport_idx_o == {k[2:0], k[5:3]} (the y-pass un-transpose permutation) and
// must equal the expected output at that row-major position. Comparison is
// EXACT (integer outputs).
module tb;
  int total_checks;
  int total_errors;

  logic        clk;
  logic        rst;
  logic        img_start;
  logic        inport_valid;
  logic [15:0] inport_data0, inport_data1, inport_data2, inport_data3;
  logic [2:0]  inport_idx;
  logic        outport_valid;
  logic [31:0] outport_data;
  logic [5:0]  outport_idx;

  jpeg_idct_2d dut (
    .clk(clk), .rst(rst),
    .img_start_i(img_start), .img_end_i(1'b0),
    .inport_valid_i(inport_valid),
    .inport_data0_i(inport_data0), .inport_data1_i(inport_data1),
    .inport_data2_i(inport_data2), .inport_data3_i(inport_data3),
    .inport_idx_i(inport_idx),
    .outport_valid_o(outport_valid),
    .outport_data_o(outport_data),
    .outport_idx_o(outport_idx)
  );

  initial clk = 1'b0;
  always #5 clk = ~clk;

  // ---- load vectors.dat: flat token stream, 128 tokens per block ----------
  localparam MAX_BLOCKS = 1024;
  logic [15:0] coeffs [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  logic [31:0] expy   [0:MAX_BLOCKS*64-1];
  int num_blocks;

  task automatic load_vectors;
    int fd, rc, n;
    logic [31:0] tok;
    logic [31:0] flat [0:MAX_BLOCKS*128-1];
    fd = $fopen("vectors.dat", "r");
    if (fd == 0) begin
      $display("ERROR: cannot open vectors.dat");
      $fatal(1);
    end
    n = 0;
    while (!$feof(fd)) begin
      rc = $fscanf(fd, "%h", tok);
      if (rc == 1) begin
        flat[n] = tok;
        n = n + 1;
      end
    end
    $fclose(fd);
    num_blocks = n / 128;
    if (num_blocks * 128 != n)
      $display("WARNING: %0d trailing tokens ignored", n - num_blocks * 128);
    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++)
      for (int i = 0; i < 64; i++) begin
        coeffs[b*64+i] = flat[b*128+i][15:0];
        expy[b*64+i]   = flat[b*128+64+i];
      end
  endtask

  // ---- output checker -------------------------------------------------------
  int out_cnt;
  bit checking;
  logic [5:0] k;

  always @(posedge clk) begin
    #1;
    if (checking && outport_valid) begin
      total_checks = total_checks + 1;
      k = out_cnt % 64;
      if (outport_idx !== {k[2:0], k[5:3]}) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d out=%0d idx=%0d expected_idx=%0d",
                 out_cnt / 64, out_cnt % 64, outport_idx, {k[2:0], k[5:3]});
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
      if (outport_data !== expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx]) begin
        $display("TB_ERROR block=%0d pos=%0d expected_y=%08x actual_y=%08x",
                 out_cnt / 64, outport_idx, expy[(out_cnt / 64) * 64 + outport_idx],
                 outport_data);
        total_errors = total_errors + 1;
      end
`ifdef DUMP_Y
      $display("Y %02x %08x", outport_idx, outport_data);
`endif
      out_cnt = out_cnt + 1;
    end
  end

  // ---- driver ----------------------------------------------------------------
  task automatic drive_beat(input logic valid, input logic [2:0] kk,
                            input logic [15:0] d0, d1, d2, d3);
    @(negedge clk);
    inport_valid = valid;
    inport_idx   = kk;
    inport_data0 = d0;
    inport_data1 = d1;
    inport_data2 = d2;
    inport_data3 = d3;
  endtask

  initial begin
    total_checks = 0;
    total_errors = 0;
    out_cnt = 0;
    checking = 0;
    rst = 1;
    img_start = 0;
    inport_valid = 0;
    inport_idx = 0;
    inport_data0 = 0; inport_data1 = 0; inport_data2 = 0; inport_data3 = 0;

    load_vectors();

    repeat (4) @(negedge clk);
    rst = 0;
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 1;             // one-cycle image-start pulse
    @(negedge clk);
    img_start = 0;
    checking = 1;

    for (int b = 0; b < num_blocks; b++) begin
      for (int r = 0; r < 8; r++) begin
        for (int kb = 0; kb < 8; kb++) begin
          if (kb == 0 || kb == 2 || kb == 5 || kb == 7)
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+2],

... (showing lines 1-147 of 169; use offset/limit for more)

[ASSISTANT]
  -> read_file(filename='tb.sv', offset=148)

[TOOL RESULT]
[Step 6/60]
                                      coeffs[b*64+r*8+4], coeffs[b*64+r*8+6]);
          else
            drive_beat(1'b1, kb[2:0], coeffs[b*64+r*8+1], coeffs[b*64+r*8+3],
                                      coeffs[b*64+r*8+5], coeffs[b*64+r*8+7]);
        end
      end
      // inter-block gap: >= 2 idle cycles (serializer IDLE+SETUP), varied a bit
      repeat (2 + (b % 3)) drive_beat(1'b0, 3'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0);
    end
    drive_beat(1'b0, 3'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0, 16'd0);
    repeat (400) @(negedge clk);  // flush x + transpose + y pipeline

    if (out_cnt != num_blocks * 64) begin
      $display("TB_ERROR missing outputs: got %0d, expected %0d", out_cnt, num_blocks * 64);
      total_errors = total_errors + 1;
    end
    $display("TB_SUMMARY total=%0d errors=%0d", total_checks, total_errors);
    if (total_errors != 0) $fatal(1, "FAIL");
    $display("PASS");
    $finish;
  end

inport_idx_i;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q; out_stg1_idx_q <= out_stg0_idx_q;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q; out_stg2_idx_q <= out_stg1_idx_q;\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] o_s5, o_s6, o_s7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t0, o_t1, o_t2, o_t3, o_t4, o_t5, o_t6, o_t7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6_5, o_t5_6;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    o_s5 <= 0; o_s6 <= 0; o_s7 <= 0; o_t0 <= 0; o_t1 <= 0; o_t2 <= 0;\n    o_t3 <= 0; o_t4 <= 0; o_t5 <= 0; o_t6 <= 0; o_t7 <= 0;\n    o_t6_5 <= 0; o_t5_6 <= 0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    case (out_stg2_idx_q)\n    3'd0: begin o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; end\n    3'd1: begin o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1; o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3; o_t0 <= mul4; end\n    3'd2: begin o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7; end\n    3'd3: begin o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3); end\n    3'd4: begin o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3; end\n    3'd5: begin o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; o_t4 <= mul4; o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5; o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6; end\n    3'd6: begin o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3; o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3; o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5; o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6; end\n    default: begin o_s5 <= (o_t6_5 * 181) / 256; o_s6 <= (o_t5_6 * 181) / 256; end\n    endcase\nend\n\nreg        out_stg3_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg3_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q; out_stg3_idx_q <= out_stg2_idx_q;\nend\n\n// x-pass output bound (worst case |value| < 2^18.9) -> 20-bit signed exact\nreg signed [19:0] block_out [0:7];\nreg signed [19:0] block_out_tmp;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_out[0] <= 0; block_out[1] <= 0; block_out[2] <= 0; block_out[3] <= 0;\n    block_out[4] <= 0; block_out[5] <= 0; block_out[6] <= 0; block_out[7] <= 0;\n    block_out_tmp <= 0;\nend\nelse if (out_stg3_valid_q)\nbegin\n    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)\n    begin\n        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)\n        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)\n    begin\n        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */\nend\n\nreg [7:0] valid_q;\nreg [5:0] ptr_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse\n    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (outport_valid_o)\n    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;\n\nassign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];\nassign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];\nassign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Double-buffered corner turn (4 x (2 x 16-entry) RAMs)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jt_ram\n#(\n     parameter W = 20\n)\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           wr_en\n    ,input           block_wr\n    ,input  [  3:0]  wr_addr\n    ,input  [W-1:0]  wr_data\n    ,input           block_rd\n    ,input  [  3:0]  rd_addr\n    ,output reg [W-1:0]  rd_data\n);\n\nreg [W-1:0] mem0 [0:15];\nreg [W-1:0] mem1 [0:15];\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (wr_en)\n    begin\n        if (block_wr)\n            mem1[wr_addr] <= wr_data;\n        else\n            mem0[wr_addr] <= wr_data;\n    end\n\n    rd_data <= block_rd ? mem1[rd_addr] : mem0[rd_addr];\nend\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule jpeg_idct_transpose\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data_i\n    ,input  [  5:0]  inport_idx_i\n    ,input           outport_ready_i\n    ,output          inport_accept_o\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data0_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data1_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data2_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data3_o\n    ,output [  2:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\nreg         block_wr_q;\nreg         block_rd_q;\nreg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;\nreg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;\nreg [3:0]   rda0_q, rda1_q, rda2_q, rda3_q;\n\nwire [3:0]  wr_addr_w = inport_idx_i[3:0];\n\nwire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);\nwire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);\nwire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);\nwire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram0\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr0_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda0_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data0_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram1\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr1_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda1_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data1_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram2\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr2_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda2_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data2_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram3\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr3_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda3_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data3_o)\n);\n\nreg [1:0] block_ready_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q    <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q    <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse if (img_start_i)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q    <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q    <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;\n        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;\n    end\n\n    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;\n        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;\n    end\nend\n\nassign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];\n\nlocalparam STATE_W           = 2;\nlocalparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;\nlocalparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;\nlocalparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;\n\nreg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;\nreg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;\n\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    next_state_r = state_q;\n    case (state_q)\n    STATE_IDLE:\n        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)\n            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;\n    STATE_SETUP:\n        next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;\n    STATE_ACTIVE:\n        if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63)\n            next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\n    default: ;\n    endcase\n    if (img_start_i)\n        next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    state_q <= STATE_IDLE;\nelse\n    state_q <= next_state_r;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;\nelse if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)\n    rd_idx_q <= rd_idx_q + 6'd1;\n\n// read address (identical schedule to original), replicated per RAM to\n// cut select-net fanout\nreg [3:0] rda_next;\n\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    case (state_q)\n    STATE_IDLE:\n        rda_next = 4'd0;\n    STATE_SETUP:\n        rda_next = 4'd8;\n    STATE_ACTIVE:\n    begin\n        case (rd_idx_q[2:0])\n        3'd0: rda_next = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd1: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        3'd2: rda_next = rd_addr_q;\n        3'd3: rda_next = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd4: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        3'd5: rda_next = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd6: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd1;\n        3'd7: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        endcase\n    end\n    default:\n        rda_next = rd_addr_q;\n    endcase\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda0_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda1_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda2_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda3_q <= 4'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    rd_addr_q <= rda_next;\n    rda0_q <= rda_next;\n    rda1_q <= rda_next;\n    rda2_q <= rda_next;\n    rda3_q <= rda_next;\nend\n\nassign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);\nassign outport_idx_o   = rd_idx_q[2:0];\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// 1-D IDCT, column pass (inputs are the 20-bit x-pass results)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_y\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data0_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data1_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data2_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data3_i\n    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o\n    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\nlocalparam OUT_SHIFT = 15;\n\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C1_16 = 13'sd4017;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C2_16 = 13'sd3784;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C3_16 = 13'sd3406;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C4_16 = 13'sd2896;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C5_16 = 13'sd2276;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C6_16 = 13'sd1567;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C7_16 = 13'sd799;\n\nwire signed [19:0] block_in_0_1 = inport_data0_i;\nwire signed [19:0] block_in_2_3 = inport_data1_i;\nwire signed [19:0] block_in_4_5 = inport_data2_i;\nwire signed [19:0] block_in_6_7 = inport_data3_i;\n\nreg signed [20:0] i0;\nreg signed [19:0] mul0_a, mul1_a, mul2_a, mul3_a;\nreg signed [20:0] mul4_a;\nreg signed [12:0] mul0_b, mul1_b, mul2_b, mul3_b, mul4_b;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    i0 <= 21'b0;\n    mul0_a <= 20'b0; mul0_b <= 13'b0;\n    mul1_a <= 20'b0; mul1_b <= 13'b0;\n    mul2_a <= 20'b0; mul2_b <= 13'b0;\n    mul3_a <= 20'b0; mul3_b <= 13'b0;\n    mul4_a <= 21'b0; mul4_b <= 13'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */\n    case (inport_idx_i)\n    3'd0:\n    begin\n        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; mul0_b <= C2_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C6_16;\n    end\n    3'd1:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C1_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C7_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C5_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C3_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0;           mul4_b <= C4_16;\n    end\n    3'd2:\n    begin\n        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;\n    end\n    3'd3:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd4:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd5:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; mul0_b <= C6_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C2_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0;           mul4_b <= C4_16;\n    end\n    default:\n        ;\n    endcase\n    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */\nend\n\n// full narrow product, then take low 32 bits: identical to the original\n// 32-bit wrapping multiply for every input value\nwire signed [32:0] prod0 = mul0_a * mul0_b;\nwire signed [32:0] prod1 = mul1_a * mul1_b;\nwire signed [32:0] prod2 = mul2_a * mul2_b;\nwire signed [32:0] prod3 = mul3_a * mul3_b;\nwire signed [33:0] prod4 = mul4_a * mul4_b;\n\nreg signed [31:0] mul0_q, mul1_q, mul2_q, mul3_q, mul4_q;\nreg signed [31:0] mul0, mul1, mul2, mul3, mul4;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    mul0_q <= 32'b0; mul1_q <= 32'b0; mul2_q <= 32'b0; mul3_q <= 32'b0; mul4_q <= 32'b0;\n    mul0 <= 32'b0; mul1 <= 32'b0; mul2 <= 32'b0; mul3 <= 32'b0; mul4 <= 32'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */\n    mul0_q <= prod0[31:0];\n    mul1_q <= prod1[31:0];\n    mul2_q <= prod2[31:0];\n    mul3_q <= prod3[31:0];\n    mul4_q <= prod4[31:0];\n    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */\n    mul0 <= mul0_q; mul1 <= mul1_q; mul2 <= mul2_q; mul3 <= mul3_q; mul4 <= mul4_q;\nend\n\nreg        out_stg0_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg1_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg2_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg0_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg1_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg2_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i; out_stg0_idx_q <= inport_idx_i;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q; out_stg1_idx_q <= out_stg0_idx_q;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q; out_stg2_idx_q <= out_stg1_idx_q;\nend\n\nreg signed [31:0] o_s5, o_s6, o_s7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t0, o_t1, o_t2, o_t3, o_t4, o_t5, o_t6, o_t7;\nreg signed [31:0] o_t6_5, o_t5_6;\n\n// (x * 181) / 256 with signed truncation toward zero, computed exactly as\n// arithmetic shift + fixup (bit-exact for every 32-bit operand value)\nwire signed [31:0] p5 = o_t6_5 * 181;\nwire signed [31:0] p6 = o_t5_6 * 181;\nwire        fix5 = p5[31] && (p5[7:0] != 8'd0);\nwire        fix6 = p6[31] && (p6[7:0] != 8'd0);\nwire signed [31:0] div5 = (p5 >>> 8) + (fix5 ? 32'sd1 : 32'sd0);\nwire signed [31:0] div6 = (p6 >>> 8) + (fix6 ? 32'sd1 : 32'sd0);\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    o_s5 <= 0; o_s6 <= 0; o_s7 <= 0; o_t0 <= 0; o_t1 <= 0; o_t2 <= 0;\n    o_t3 <= 0; o_t4 <= 0; o_t5 <= 0; o_t6 <= 0; o_t7 <= 0;\n    o_t6_5 <= 0; o_t5_6 <= o_t3; o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7; end\n    3'd3: begin o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3); end\n    3'd4: begin o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3; end\n    3'd5: begin o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; o_t4 <= mul4; o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5; o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6; end\n    3'd6: begin o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3; o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3; o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5; o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6; end\n    default: begin o_s5 <= div5; o_s6 <= div6; end\n    endcase\nend\n\nreg        out_stg3_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg3_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q; out_stg3_idx_q <= out_stg2_idx_q;\nend\n\n// y-pass output bound: 32-bit wrapped sum >> 15 always fits 17-bit signed\nreg signed [16:0] block_out [0:7];\nreg signed [16:0] block_out_tmp;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_out[0] <= 0; block_out[1] <= 0; block_out[2] <= 0; block_out[3] <= 0;\n    block_out[4] <= 0; block_out[5] <= 0; block_out[6] <= 0; block_out[7] <= 0;\n    block_out_tmp <= 0;\nend\nelse if (out_stg3_valid_q)\nbegin\n    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)\n    begin\n        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)\n        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)\n    begin\n        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - o_s5) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + o_s6) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - [ASSISTANT]
v2 passed but got **slower** (3446 ps) — the RAM read mux is the bottleneck: the read-address bits drive ~1280 mux-select loads each. v3: replace the mux tree with a **one-hot decoded AND-OR read structure** (decode rd_addr → 32 gated one-hot lines, balanced OR-tree per bit). Fanout per net drops from ~1000 to ~20, and the tree is 5 levels instead of a deep mux chain.

[ASSISTANT]
Big win: **5.75M** (was 13.05M). New critical path: `o_t5_6 → *181 → fixup-detect (NOR5 chain, ~420ps) → mux → o_s6` in the x-pass. Since 181 is odd, `(x*181) mod 256 == 0 ⟺ x[7:0] == 0`, and the product's sign bit equals `x[31]` — so the fixup term can be computed from the input register alone (tiny logic) and folded as a `+255·fix` partial product into the multiply's adder tree, eliminating both the post-multiply fixup chain and the extra increment adder. This is bit-exact for every input.

[ASSISTANT]
The fold was inexact for large operands (|x·181| ≥ 2³¹ flips the product sign bit — 3 vector misses). Exact fix, variant A: keep the true 32-bit product, but (a) compute the low-byte-zero flag **early and cheaply** from the operand (`(x·181) mod 256 == (x[7:0]·181) mod 256`, since 181 is odd), (b) register `(p>>>8)` and the fix bit **separately** and fold the fix into the downstream 3-operand add — eliminating both the post-multiply 8-input NOR chain and the 32-bit increment from the critical cycle. This is bit-exact for **all** inputs.

// x-pass output bound (worst case over all 16-bit inputs < 2^19) -> 20-bit
reg signed [19:0] block_out [0:7];
reg signed [19:0] block_out_tmp;

always @ (posedge clk)
if (rst)
begin
    block_out[0] <= 0; block_out[1] <= 0; block_out[2] <= 0; block_out[3] <= 0;
    block_out[4] <= 0; block_out[5] <= 0; block_out[6] <= 0; block_out[7] <= 0;
    block_out_tmp <= 0;
end
else if (out_stg3_valid_q)
begin
    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */
    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)
    begin
        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);
    end

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)
        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;

    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)
    begin
        block_out[2] <= (o_t2 + s5hi_q + f5_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT;
        block_out[5] <= (o_t2 - s5hi_q - f5_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT;
        block_out[1] <= (o_t1 + s6hi_q + f6_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT;
        block_out[6] <= (o_t1 - s6hi_q - f6_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT;
    end
    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */
end

reg [7:0] valid_q;
reg [5:0] ptr_q;

always @ (posedge clk)
if (rst)
    valid_q <= 8'b0;
else if (img_start_i)
    valid_q <= 8'b0;
else
    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};

always @ (posedge clk)
if (rst)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (img_start_i)
    ptr_q <= 6'd0;
else if (outport_valid_o)
    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;

assign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];
assign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];
assign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;

endmodule

//--- 1/sqrt(2) scaling: d = trunc((x*181 mod 2^32)/256) (signed, exact) ------
// p = (x*181) mod 2^32 ; d = (p >>> 8) + (p[31] && p[7:0]!=0)
// p[7:0] = (x[7:0]*181) mod 256 (181 odd) -> zero detect computed early.
wire signed [31:0] px5 = o_t6_5 * 181;
wire signed [31:0] px6 = o_t5_6 * 181;
wire        nzx5 = ((o_t6_5[7:0] * 8'd181) != 8'd0);
wire        nzx6 = ((o_t5_6[7:0] * 8'd181) != 8'd0);

reg signed [23:0] s5hi_q, s6hi_q;   // px >>> 8 (fits 24-bit signed)
reg        f5_q, f6_q;              // truncation fixup bit

always @ (posedge clk)
if (rst)
begin
    s5hi_q <= 24'b0; s6hi_q <= 24'b0; f5_q <= 1'b0; f6_q <= 1'b0;
end
else if (out_stg2_idx_q == 3'd7)
begin
    s5hi_q <= px5[31:8];
    s6hi_q <= px6[31:8];
    f5_q   <= px5[31] & nzx5;
    f6_q   <= px6[31] & nzx6;
end

always @ (posedge clk)
if (rst)
begin
    o_s5 <= 0; o_s6 <= 0; o_s7 <= 0; o_t0 <= 0; o_t1 <= 0; o_t2 <= 0;
    o_t3 <= 0; o_t4 <= 0; o_t5 <= 0; o_t6 <= 0; o_t7 <= 0;
    o_t6_5 <= 0; o_t5_6 <= 0;
end
else
begin
    case (out_stg2_idx_q)
    3'd0: begin o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; end
    3'd1: begin o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1; o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3; o_t0 <= mul4; end
    3'd2: begin o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7; end
    3'd3: begin o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3); end
    3'd4: begin o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3; end
    3'd5: begin o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; o_t4 <= mul4; o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5; o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6; end
    3'd6: begin o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3; o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3; o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5; o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6; end
    default: ;
    endcase
end

reg        out_stg3_valid_q;
reg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;

always @ (posedge clk)
if (rst)
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg3_idx_q <= 3'b0;
end
else
begin
    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q; out_stg3_idx_q <= out_stg2_idx_q;
end

prod0 = mul0_a * mul0_b;\nwire signed [27:0] prod1 = mul1_a * mul1_b;\nwire signed [27:0] prod2 = mul2_a * mul2_b;\nwire signed [27:0] prod3 = mul3_a * mul3_b;\nwire signed [28:0] prod4 = mul4_a * mul4_b;\n\nreg signed [27:0] mul0_q, mul1_q, mul2_q, mul3_q;\nreg signed [28:0] mul4_q;\nreg signed [27:0] mul0, mul1, mul2, mul3;\nreg signed [28:0] mul4;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    mul0_q <= 28'b0; mul1_q <= 28'b0; mul2_q <= 28'b0; mul3_q <= 28'b0; mul4_q <= 29'b0;\n    mul0 <= 28'b0; mul1 <= 28'b0; mul2 <= 28'b0; mul3 <= 28'b0; mul4 <= 29'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    mul0_q <= prod0;\n    mul1_q <= prod1;\n    mul2_q <= prod2;\n    mul3_q <= prod3;\n    mul4_q <= prod4;\n    mul0 <= mul0_q; mul1 <= mul1_q; mul2 <= mul2_q; mul3 <= mul3_q; mul4 <= mul4_q;\nend\n\nreg        out_stg0_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg1_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg2_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg0_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg1_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg2_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i; out_stg0_idx_q <= inport_idx_i;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q; out_stg1_idx_q <= out_stg0_idx_q;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q; out_stg2_idx_q <= out_stg1_idx_q;\nend\n\n// all x-pass internal quantities bounded by 870.4M < 2^30 -> 31-bit signed\nreg signed [30:0] o_s5, o_s6, o_s7;\nreg signed [30:0] o_t0, o_t1, o_t2, o_t3, o_t4, o_t5, o_t6, o_t7;\nreg signed [30:0] o_t6_5, o_t5_6;\n\n//--- 1/sqrt(2) scaling: d = trunc((x*181 mod 2^32)/256) (signed, exact) ------\n// p = (x*181) mod 2^32 ; d = (p >>> 8) + (p[31] && p[7:0]!=0)\n// p[7:0] = (x[7:0]*181) mod 256 (181 odd) -> zero detect computed early.\nwire signed [31:0] px5 = o_t6_5 * 181;\nwire signed [31:0] px6 = o_t5_6 * 181;\nwire        nzx5 = ((o_t6_5[7:0] * 8'd181) != 8'd0);\nwire        nzx6 = ((o_t5_6[7:0] * 8'd181) != 8'd0);\n\nreg signed [23:0] s5hi_q, s6hi_q;   // p >>> 8 (fits 24-bit signed)\nreg signed [31:0] f5_q, f6_q;       // truncation fixup (0 or +1), signed\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    s5hi_q <= 24'b0; s6hi_q <= 24'b0; f5_q <= 32'b0; f6_q <= 32'b0;\nend\nelse if (out_stg2_idx_q == 3'd7)\nbegin\n    s5hi_q <= px5[31:8];\n    s6hi_q <= px6[31:8];\n    f5_q   <= px5[31] & nzx5;\n    f6_q   <= px6[31] & nzx6;\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    o_s5 <= 0; o_s6 <= 0; o_s7 <= 0; o_t0 <= 0; o_t1 <= 0; o_t2 <= 0;\n    o_t3 <= 0; o_t4 <= 0; o_t5 <= 0; o_t6 <= 0; o_t7 <= 0;\n    o_t6_5 <= 0; o_t5_6 <= 0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    case (out_stg2_idx_q)\n    3'd0: begin o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; end\n    3'd1: begin o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1; o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3; o_t0 <= mul4; end\n    3'd2: begin o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7; end\n    3'd3: begin o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3); end\n    3'd4: begin o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3; end\n    3'd5: begin o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; o_t4 <= mul4; o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5; o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6; end\n    3'd6: begin o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3; o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3; o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5; o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6; end\n    default: ;\n    endcase\nend\n\nreg        out_stg3_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg3_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q; out_stg3_idx_q <= out_stg2_idx_q;\nend\n\n// |out| <= 178310 < 2^18 -> 18-bit exact\nreg signed [17:0] block_out [0:7];\nreg signed [17:0] block_out_tmp;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_out[0] <= 0; block_out[1] <= 0; block_out[2] <= 0; block_out[3] <= 0;\n    block_out[4] <= 0; block_out[5] <= 0; block_out[6] <= 0; block_out[7] <= 0;\n    block_out_tmp <= 0;\nend\nelse if (out_stg3_valid_q)\nbegin\n    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)\n    begin\n        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)\n        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)\n    begin\n        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + s5hi_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - s5hi_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + s6hi_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - s6hi_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */\nend\n\nreg [7:0] valid_q;\nreg [5:0] ptr_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse\n    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (outport_valid_o)\n    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;\n\nassign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];\nassign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];\nassign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Double-buffered corner turn (4 lanes x 2 halves x 16 entries)\n// Read path: one-hot decode + AND-OR tree (low fanout, shallow).\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jt_ram\n#(\n     parameter W = 18\n)\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           wr_en\n    ,input           block_wr\n    ,input  [  3:0]  wr_addr\n    ,input  [W-1:0]  wr_data\n    ,input           block_rd\n    ,input  [  3:0]  rd_addr\n    ,output reg [W-1:0]  rd_data\n);\n\nreg [W-1:0] mem0 [0:15];\nreg [W-1:0] mem1 [0:15];\n\n// one-hot decode of the read address\nreg [15:0] oh;\ninteger i;\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    for (i = 0; i < 16; i = i + 1)\n        oh[i] = (rd_addr == i);\nend\n\nwire [15:0] oh0 = oh & {16{~block_rd}};\nwire [15:0] oh1 = oh & {16{ block_rd}};\n\n// gated words: exactly one of the 32 is (possibly) non-zero\nreg [W-1:0] gt [0:31];\nreg [W-1:0] t1 [0:15];\nreg [W-1:0] t2 [0:7];\nreg [W-1:0] t3 [0:3];\nreg [W-1:0] t4 [0:1];\nreg [W-1:0] acc;\ninteger k;\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1)\n    begin\n        gt[k]    = mem0[k] & {W{oh0[k]}};\n        gt[k+16] = mem1[k] & {W{oh1[k]}};\n    end\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1) t1[k] = gt[2*k] | gt[2*k+1];\n    for (k = 0; k < 8;  k = k + 1) t2[k] = t1[2*k] | t1[2*k+1];\n    for (k = 0; k < 4;  k = k + 1) t3[k] = t2[2*k] | t2[2*k+1];\n    for (k = 0; k < 2;  k = k + 1) t4[k] = t3[2*k] | t3[2*k+1];\n    acc = t4[0] | t4[1];\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (wr_en)\n    begin\n        if (block_wr)\n            mem1[wr_addr] <= wr_data;\n        else\n            mem0[wr_addr] <= wr_data;\n    end\n\n    rd_data <= acc;\nend\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule jpeg_idct_transpose\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [ 17:0]  inport_data_i\n    ,input  [  5:0]  inport_idx_i\n    ,input           outport_ready_i\n    ,output          inport_accept_o\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [ 17:0]  outport_data0_o\n    ,output [ 17:0]  outport_data1_o\n    ,output [ 17:0]  outport_data2_o\n    ,output [ 17:0]  outport_data3_o\n    ,output [  2:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\nreg         block_wr_q;\nreg         block_rd_q;\nreg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;\nreg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;\nreg [3:0]   rda0_q, rda1_q, rda2_q, rda3_q;\n\nwire [3:0]  wr_addr_w = inport_idx_i[3:0];\n\nwire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);\nwire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);\nwire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);\nwire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(18))\nu_ram0\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr0_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda0_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data0_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(18))\nu_ram1\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr1_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda1_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data1_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(18))\nu_ram2\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr2_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda2_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data2_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(18))\nu_ram3\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr3_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda3_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data3_o)\n);\n\nreg [1:0] block_ready_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q    <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q    <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse if (img_start_i)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q    <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q    <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;\n        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;\n    end\n\n    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;\n        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;\n    end\nend\n\nassign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];\n\nlocalparam STATE_W           = 2;\nlocalparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;\nlocalparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;\nlocalparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;\n\nreg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;\nreg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;\n\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    next_state_r = state_q;\n    case (state_q)\n    STATE_IDLE:\n        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)\n            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;\n    STATE_SETUP:\n        next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;\n    STATE_ACTIVE:\n        if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63)\n            next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\n    default: ;\n    endcase\n    if (img_start_i)\n        next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    state_q <= STATE_IDLE;\nelse\n    state_q <= next_state_r;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;\nelse if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)\n    rd_idx_q <= rd_idx_q + 6'd1;\n\n// read address (identical schedule to original), replicated per RAM to\n// cut select-net fanout\nreg [3:0] rda_next;\n\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    case (state_q)\n    STATE_IDLE:\n        rda_next = 4'd0;\n    STATE_SETUP:\n        rda_next = 4'd8;\n    STATE_ACTIVE:\n    begin\n        case (rd_idx_q[2:0])\n        3'd0: rda_next = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd1: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        3'd2: rda_next = rd_addr_q;\n        3'd3: rda_next = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd4: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        3'd5: rda_next = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd6: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd1;\n        3'd7: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        endcase\n    end\n    default:\n        rda_next = rd_addr_q;\n    endcase\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda0_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda1_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda2_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda3_q <= 4'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    rd_addr_q <= rda_next;\n    rda0_q <= rda_next;\n    rda1_q <= rda_next;\n    rda2_q <= rda_next;\n    rda3_q <= rda_next;\nend\n\nassign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);\nassign outport_idx_o   = rd_idx_q[2:0];\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// 1-D IDCT, column pass (inputs are the 18-bit x-pass results)\n// |in| <= 178310 -> |p| <= 716.3M < 2^30 (31-bit exact products)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_y\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [ 17:0]  inport_data0_i\n    ,input  [ 17:0]  inport_data1_i\n    ,input  [ 17:0]  inport_data2_i\n    ,input  [ 17:0]  inport_data3_i\n    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o\n    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\nlocalparam OUT_SHIFT = 15;\n\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C1_16 = 13'sd4017;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C2_16 = 13'sd3784;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C3_16 = 13'sd3406;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C4_16 = 13'sd2896;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C5_16 = 13'sd2276;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C6_16 = 13'sd1567;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C7_16 = 13'sd799;\n\nwire signed [17:0] block_in_0_1 = inport_data0_i;\nwire signed [17:0] block_in_2_3 = inport_data1_i;\nwire signed [17:0] block_in_4_5 = inport_data2_i;\nwire signed [17:0] block_in_6_7 = inport_data3_i;\n\nreg signed [18:0] i0;\nreg signed [17:0] mul0_a, mul1_a, mul2_a, mul3_a;\nreg signed [18:0] mul4_a;\nreg signed [12:0] mul0_b, mul1_b, mul2_b, mul3_b, mul4_b;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    i0 <= 19'b0;\n    mul0_a <= 18'b0; mul0_b <= 13'b0;\n    mul1_a <= 18'b0; mul1_b <= 13'b0;\n    mul2_a <= 18'b0; mul2_b <= 13'b0;\n    mul3_a <= 18'b0; mul3_b <= 13'b0;\n    mul4_a <= 19'b0; mul4_b <= 13'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */\n    case (inport_idx_i)\n    3'd0:\n    begin\n        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; mul0_b <= C2_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C6_16;\n    end\n    3'd1:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C1_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C7_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C5_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C3_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0;           mul4_b <= C4_16;\n    end\n    3'd2:\n    begin\n        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;\n    end\n    3'd3:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd4:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd5:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; mul0_b <= C6_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C2_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0;           mul4_b <= C4_16;\n    end\n    default:\n        ;\n    endcase\n    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */\nend\n\n// |p| <= 178310*4017 = 716.3M < 2^30 (31-bit exact); |i0*C4| <= 1032.8M < 2^30\nwire signed [30:0] prod0 = mul0_a * mul0_b;\nwire signed [30:0] prod1 = mul1_a * mul1_b;\nwire signed [30:0] prod2 = mul2_a * mul2_b;\nwire signed [30:0] prod3 = mul3_a * mul3_b;\nwire signed [30:0] prod4 = mul4_a * mul4_b;\n\nreg signed [30:0] mul0_q, mul1_q, mul2_q, mul3_q, mul4_q;\nreg signed [30:0] mul0, mul1, mul2, mul3, mul4;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    mul0_q <= 31'b0; mul1_q <= 31'b0; mul2_q <= 31'b0; mul3_q <= 31'b0; mul4_q <= 31'b0;\n    mul0 <= 31'b0; mul1 <= 31'b0; mul2 <= 31'b0; mul3 <= 31'b0; mul4 <= 31'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    mul0_q <= prod0;\n    mul1_q <= prod1;\n    mul2_q <= prod2;\n    mul3_q <= prod3;\n    mul4_q <= prod4;\n    mul0 <= mul0_q; mul1 <= mul1_q; mul2 <= mul2_q; mul3 <= mul3_q; mul4 <= mul4_q;\nend\n\nreg        out_stg0_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg1_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg2_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg0_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg1_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg2_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i; out_stg0_idx_q <= inport_idx_i;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q; out_stg1_idx_q <= out_stg0_idx_q;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q; out_stg2_idx_q <= out_stg1_idx_q;\nend\n\n// 32-bit wrapping context preserved (some sums can exceed 2^31, as original)\nreg signed [31:0] o_s5, o_s6, block_out[0] <= 0; block_out[1] <= 0; block_out[2] <= 0; block_out[3] <= 0;\n    block_out[4] <= 0; block_out[5] <= 0; block_out[6] <= 0; block_out[7] <= 0;\n    block_out_tmp <= 0;\nend\nelse if o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)\n        block_out[7]
