C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= d2; mul3_b <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd5:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= d2; mul0_b <= C6_16;\n        mul1_a <= d6; mul1_b <= C2_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0; mul4_b <= C4_16;\n    end\n    default:\n        ;\n    endcase\n    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */\nend\n\n// 2-stage split of each product a*b (a = 16/17-bit signed, b = 13-bit const):\n// stage 1 registers lo = a[7:0]*b and hi = a[15:8]*b (hi signed);\n// stage 2 forms lo + (hi << 8) = a*b exactly (no wrap at these widths).\nwire signed [20:0] p0lo = $signed({1'b0, mul0_a[7:0]}) * mul0_b;\nwire signed [19:0] p0hi = $signed(mul0_a[15:8]) * mul0_b;\nwire signed [20:0] p1lo = $signed({1'b0, mul1_a[7:0]}) * mul1_b;\nwire signed [19:0] p1hi = $signed(mul1_a[15:8]) * mul1_b;\nwire signed [20:0] p2lo = $signed({1'b0, mul2_a[7:0]}) * mul2_b;\nwire signed [19:0] p2hi = $signed(mul2_a[15:8]) * mul2_b;\nwire signed [20:0] p3lo = $signed({1'b0, mul3_a[7:0]}) * mul3_b;\nwire signed [19:0] p3hi = $signed(mul3_a[15:8]) * mul3_b;\nwire signed [20:0] p4lo = $signed({1'b0, mul4_a[7:0]}) * mul4_b;\nwire signed [20:0] p4hi = $signed(mul4_a[16:8]) * mul4_b;\n\nreg signed [20:0] lo0_q, lo1_q, lo2_q, lo3_q, lo4_q;\nreg signed [19:0] hi0_q, hi1_q, hi2_q, hi3_q, hi4_q;\n\nreg signed [27:0] mul0, mul1, mul2, mul3;\nreg signed [28:0] mul4;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    lo0_q <= 0; lo1_q <= 0; lo2_q <= 0; lo3_q <= 0; lo4_q <= 0;\n    hi0_q <= 0; hi1_q <= 0; hi2_q <= 0; hi3_q <= 0; hi4_q <= 0;\n    mul0 <= 0; mul1 <= 0; mul2 <= 0; mul3 <= 0; mul4 <= 0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    lo0_q <= p0lo; hi0_q <= p0hi;\n    lo1_q <= p1lo; hi1_q <= p1hi;\n    lo2_q <= p2lo; hi2_q <= p2hi;\n    lo3_q <= p3lo; hi3_q <= p3hi;\n    lo4_q <= p4lo; hi4_q <= p4hi;\n    mul0 <= lo0_q + (hi0_q <<< 8);\n    mul1 <= lo1_q + (hi1_q <<< 8);\n    mul2 <= lo2_q + (hi2_q <<< 8);\n    mul3 <= lo3_q + (hi3_q <<< 8);\n    mul4 <= lo4_q + (hi4_q <<< 8);\nend\n\nreg        out_stg0_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg1_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg2_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg0_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg1_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg2_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i; out_stg0_idx_q <= inport_idx_i;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q; out_stg1_idx_q <= out_stg0_idx_q;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q; out_stg2_idx_q <= out_stg1_idx_q;\nend\n\n// all x-pass internals bounded by 570.7M < 2^30 -> 31-bit signed\nreg signed [30:0] o_s5, o_s6, o_s7;\nreg signed [30:0] o_t0, o_t1, o_t2, o_t3, o_t4, o_t5, o_t6, o_t7;\nreg signed [30:0] o_t6_5, o_t5_6;\n\n//--- 1/sqrt(2) scaling: d = trunc((x*181 mod 2^32)/256) (signed, exact) ------\n// p = (x*181) mod 2^32 ; d = (p >>> 8) + (p[31] && p[7:0]!=0)\n// p[7:0] = (x[7:0]*181) mod 256 (181 odd) -> zero detect computed early.\nwire signed [31:0] px5 = o_t6_5 * 181;\nwire signed [31:0] px6 = o_t5_6 * 181;\nwire        nzx5 = ((o_t6_5[7:0] * 8'd181) != 8'd0);\nwire        nzx6 = ((o_t5_6[7:0] * 8'd181) != 8'd0);\n\nreg signed [23:0] s5hi_q, s6hi_q;   // p >>> 8 (fits 24-bit signed)\nreg signed [31:0] f5_q, f6_q;       // truncation fixup (0 or +1), signed\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    s5hi_q <= 24'b0; s6hi_q <= 24'b0; f5_q <= 32'b0; f6_q <= 32'b0;\nend\nelse if (out_stg2_idx_q == 3'd7)\nbegin\n    s5hi_q <= px5[31:8];\n    s6hi_q <= px6[31:8];\n    f5_q   <= px5[31] & nzx5;\n    f6_q   <= px6[31] & nzx6;\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    o_s5 <= 0; o_s6 <= 0; o_s7 <= 0; o_t0 <= 0; o_t1 <= 0; o_t2 <= 0;\n    o_t3 <= 0; o_t4 <= 0; o_t5 <= 0; o_t6 <= 0; o_t7 <= 0;\n    o_t6_5 <= 0; o_t5_6 <= 0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    case (out_stg2_idx_q)\n    3'd0: begin o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; end\n    3'd1: begin o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1; o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3; o_t0 <= mul4; end\n    3'd2: begin o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7; end\n    3'd3: begin o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3); end\n    3'd4: begin o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3; end\n    3'd5: begin o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; o_t4 <= mul4; o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5; o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6; end\n    3'd6: begin o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3; o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3; o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5; o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6; end\n    default: ;\n    endcase\nend\n\nreg        out_stg3_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg3_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q; out_stg3_idx_q <= out_stg2_idx_q;\nend\n\n// |out| <= 346243 < 2^19 -> 20-bit exact\nreg signed [19:0] block_out [0:7];\nreg signed [19:0] block_out_tmp;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_out[0] <= 0; block_out[1] <= 0; block_out[2] <= 0; block_out[3] <= 0;\n    block_out[4] <= 0; block_out[5] <= 0; block_out[6] <= 0; block_out[7] <= 0;\n    block_out_tmp <= 0;\nend\nelse if (out_stg3_valid_q)\nbegin\n    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)\n    begin\n        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)\n        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)\n    begin\n        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + s5hi_q + f5_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - s5hi_q - f5_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + s6hi_q + f6_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - s6hi_q - f6_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */\nend\n\nreg [7:0] valid_q;\nreg [5:0] ptr_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse\n    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (outport_valid_o)\n    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;\n\nassign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];\nassign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];\nassign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Double-buffered corner turn (4 lanes x 2 halves x 16 entries)\n// Read path: one-hot decode + AND-OR tree (low fanout, shallow).\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jt_ram\n#(\n     parameter W = 20\n)\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           wr_en\n    ,input           block_wr\n    ,input  [  3:0]  wr_addr\n    ,input  [W-1:0]  wr_data\n    ,input           block_rd\n    ,input  [  3:0]  rd_addr\n    ,output reg [W-1:0]  rd_data\n);\n\nreg [W-1:0] mem0 [0:15];\nreg [W-1:0] mem1 [0:15];\n\n// one-hot decode of the read address\nreg [15:0] oh;\ninteger i;\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    for (i = 0; i < 16; i = i + 1)\n        oh[i] = (rd_addr == i);\nend\n\nwire [15:0] oh0 = oh & {16{~block_rd}};\nwire [15:0] oh1 = oh & {16{ block_rd}};\n\n// gated words: exactly one of the 32 is (possibly) non-zero\nreg [W-1:0] gt [0:31];\nreg [W-1:0] t1 [0:15];\nreg [W-1:0] t2 [0:7];\nreg [W-1:0] t3 [0:3];\nreg [W-1:0] t4 [0:1];\nreg [W-1:0] acc;\ninteger k;\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1)\n    begin\n        gt[k]    = mem0[k] & {W{oh0[k]}};\n        gt[k+16] = mem1[k] & {W{oh1[k]}};\n    end\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1) t1[k] = gt[2*k] | gt[2*k+1];\n    for (k = 0; k < 8;  k = k + 1) t2[k] = t1[2*k] | t1[2*k+1];\n    for (k = 0; k < 4;  k = k + 1) t3[k] = t2[2*k] | t2[2*k+1];\n    for (k = 0; k < 2;  k = k + 1) t4[k] = t3[2*k] | t3[2*k+1];\n    acc = t4[0] | t4[1];\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (wr_en)\n    begin\n        if (block_wr)\n            mem1[wr_addr] <= wr_data;\n        else\n            mem0[wr_addr] <= wr_data;\n    end\n\n    rd_data <= acc;\nend\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule jpeg_idct_transpose\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data_i\n    ,input  [  5:0]  inport_idx_i\n    ,input           outport_ready_i\n    ,output          inport_accept_o\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data0_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data1_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data2_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data3_o\n    ,output [  2:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\nreg         block_wr_q;\nreg         block_rd_q;\nreg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;\nreg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;\nreg [3:0]   rda0_q, rda1_q, rda2_q, rda3_q;\n\nwire [3:0]  wr_addr_w = inport_idx_i[3:0];\n\nwire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);\nwire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);\nwire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);\nwire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram0\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr0_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda0_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data0_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram1\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr1_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda1_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data1_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram2\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr2_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda2_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data2_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram3\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr3_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda3_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data3_o)\n);\n\nreg [1:0] block_ready_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q    <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q    <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse if (img_start_i)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q    <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q    <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;\n        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;\n    end\n\n    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;\n        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;\n    end\nend\n\nassign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];\n\nlocalparam STATE_W           = 2;\nlocalparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;\nlocalparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;\nlocalparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;\n\nreg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;\nreg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;\n\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    next_state_r = state_q;\n    case (state_q)\n    STATE_IDLE:\n        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)\n            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;\n    STATE_SETUP:\n        next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;\n    STATE_ACTIVE:\n        if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63)\n            next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\n    default: ;\n    endcase\n    if (img_start_i)\n        next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    state_q <= STATE_IDLE;\nelse\n    state_q <= next_state_r;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;\nelse if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)\n    rd_idx_q <= rd_idx_q + 6'd1;\n\n// read address (identical schedule to original), replicated per RAM to\n// cut select-net fanout\nreg [3:0] rda_next;\n\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    case (state_q)\n    STATE_IDLE:\n        rda_next = 4'd0;\n    STATE_SETUP:\n        rda_next = 4'd8;\n    STATE_ACTIVE:\n    begin\n        case (rd_idx_q[2:0])\n        3'd0: rda_next = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd1: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        3'd2: rda_next = rd_addr_q;\n        3'd3: rda_next = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd4: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        3'd5: rda_next = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd6: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd1;\n        3'd7: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        endcase\n    end\n    default:\n        rda_next = rd_addr_q;\n    endcase\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda0_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda1_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda2_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda3_q <= 4'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    rd_addr_q <= rda_next;\n    rda0_q <= rda_next;\n    rda1_q <= rda_next;\n    rda2_q <= rda_next;\n    rda3_q <= rda_next;\nend\n\nassign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);\nassign outport_idx_o   = rd_idx_q[2:0];\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// 1-D IDCT, column pass (inputs are the 20-bit x-pass results, |in| <=\n// 346243). 32-bit wrapping context preserved exactly as the original.\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_y\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data0_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data1_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data2_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data3_i\n    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o\n    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\nlocalparam OUT_SHIFT = 15;\n\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C1_16 = 13'sd4017;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C2_16 = 13'sd3784;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C3_16 = 13'sd3406;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C4_16 = 13'sd2896;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C5_16 = 13'sd2276;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C6_16 = 13'sd1567;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C7_16 = 13'sd799;\n\nwire signed [19:0] block_in_0_1 = inport_data0_i;\nwire signed [19:0] block_in_2_3 = inport_data1_i;\nwire signed [19:0] block_in_4_5 = inport_data2_i;\nwire signed [19:0] block_in_6_7 = inport_data3_i;\n\nreg signed [20:0] i0;\nreg signed [19:0] mul0_a, mul1_a, mul2_a, mul3_a;\nreg signed [20:0] mul4_a;\nreg signed [12:0] mul0_b, mul1_b, mul2_b, mul3_b, mul4_b;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    i0 <= 21'b0;\n    mul0_a <= 20'b0; mul0_b <= 13'b0;\n    mul1_a <= 20'b0; mul1_b <= 13'b0;\n    mul2_a <= 20'b0; mul2_b <= 13'b0;\n    mul3_a <= 20'b0; mul3_b <= 13'b0;\n    mul4_a <= 21'b0; mul4_b <= 13'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */\n    case (inport_idx_i)\n    3'd0:\n    begin\n        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; mul0_b <= C2_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C6_16;\n    end\n    3'd1:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C1_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C7_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C5_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C3_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0;           mul4_b <= C4_16;\n    end\n    3'd2:\n    begin\n        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;\n    end\n    3'd3:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd4:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_b <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_b <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_b <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd5:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; mul0_b <= C6_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_b <= C2_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0;           mul4_b <= C4_16;\n    end\n    default:\n        ;\n    endcase\n    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */\nend\n\n// 2-stage split of each product a*b, taken mod 2^32 exactly as the original:\n// stage 1 registers lo = a[9:0]*b (zero-extended) and hi = a[19:10]*b (signed)\n// (mul4: hi = a[20:10]); stage 2 forms lo + (hi << 10) in 32-bit ,.inport_valid_i(inport_valid_i)\n    ,.inport_data0_i(inport_data0_i)\n    ,.inport_data1_i(inport_data1_i)\n    ,.inport_data2_i(inport_data2_i)\n    ,.inport_data3_i(inport_data3_i)\n    ,.inport_idx_i(inport_idx_i)\n    ,.outport_valid_o(idct_x_valid_w)\n    ,.outport_data_o(idct_x_data_w)\n    ,.outport_idx_o(idct_x_idx_w)\n);\n\nwire          transpose_valid_w;\nwire [ 19:0]  transpose_data0_w;\nwire [ 19:0]  transpose_data1_w;\nwire [ 19:0]  transpose_data2_w;\nwire [ 19:0]  transpose_data3_w;\nwire [  2:0]  transpose_idx_w;\n\njpeg_idct_transpose\nu_transpose\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.img_start_i(img_start_i)\n    ,.img_end_i(img_end_i)\n    ,.inport_valid_i(idct_x_valid_w)\n    ,.inport_data_i(idct_x_data_w)\n    ,.inport_idx_i(idct_x_idx_w)\n    ,.inport_accept_o()\n    ,.outport_valid_o(transpose_valid_w)\n    ,.outport_data0_o(transpose_data0_w)\n    ,.outport_data1_o(transpose_data1_w)\n    ,.outport_data2_o(transpose_data2_w)\n    ,.outport_data3_o(transpose_data3_w)\n    ,.outport_idx_o(transpose_idx_w)\n    ,.outport_ready_i(1'b1)\n);\n\njpeg_idct_y\nu_idct_y\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.img_start_i(img_start_i)\n    ,.img_end_i(img_end_i)\n    ,.inport_valid_i(transpose_valid_w)\n    ,.inport_data0_i(transpose_data0_w)\n    ,.inport_data1_i(transpose_data1_w)\n    ,.inport_data2_i(transpose_data2_w)\n    ,.inport_data3_i(transpose_data3_w)\n    ,.inport_idx_i(transpose_idx_w)\n    ,.outport_valid_o(outport_valid_o)\n    ,.outport_data_o(outport_data_o)\n    ,.outport_idx_o(outport_idx_o)\n);\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// 1-D IDCT, row pass (input coefficients 16-bit signed)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_x\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data0_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data1_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data2_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data3_i\n    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data_o\n    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\nlocalparam OUT_SHIFT = 11;\n\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C1_16 = 13'sd4017;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C2_16 = 13'sd3784;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C3_16 = 13'sd3406;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C4_16 = 13'sd2896;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C5_16 = 13'sd2276;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C6_16 = 13'sd1567;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C7_16 = 13'sd799;\n\nwire signed [15:0] d0 = inport_data0_i;\nwire signed [15:0] d2 = inport_data1_i;\nwire signed [15:0] d4 = inport_data2_i;\nwire signed [15:0] d6 = inport_data3_i;\n\nreg signed [16:0] i0;\nreg signed [15:0] mul0_a, mul1_a, mul2_a, mul3_a;\nreg signed [16:0] mul4_a;\n// two copies of each constant register: one feeds the lo partial-product\n// array, the other the hi array (halves the fanout on each net)\nreg signed [12:0] mul0_bl, mul0_bh;\nreg signed [12:0] mul1_bl, mul1_bh;\nreg signed [12:0] mul2_bl, mul2_bh;\nreg signed [12:0] mul3_bl, mul3_bh;\nreg signed [12:0] mul4_bl, mul4_bh;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    i0 <= 17'b0;\n    mul0_a <= 16'b0; mul0_bl <= 13'b0; mul0_bh <= 13'b0;\n    mul1_a <= 16'b0; mul1_bl <= 13'b0; mul1_bh <= 13'b0;\n    mul2_a <= 16'b0; mul2_bl <= 13'b0; mul2_bh <= 13'b0;\n    mul3_a <= 16'b0; mul3_bl <= 13'b0; mul3_bh <= 13'b0;\n    mul4_a <= 17'b0; mul4_bl <= 13'b0; mul4_bh <= 13'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */\n    case (inport_idx_i)\n    3'd0:\n    begin\n        i0     <= d0 + d4;\n        mul0_a <= d2; mul0_bl <= C2_16; mul0_bh <= C2_16;\n        mul1_a <= d6; mul1_bl <= C6_16; mul1_bh <= C6_16;\n    end\n    3'd1:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= d0; mul0_bl <= C1_16; mul0_bh <= C1_16;\n        mul1_a <= d6; mul1_bl <= C7_16; mul1_bh <= C7_16;\n        mul2_a <= d4; mul2_bl <= C5_16; mul2_bh <= C5_16;\n        mul3_a <= d2; mul3_bl <= C3_16; mul3_bh <= C3_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0; mul4_bl <= C4_16; mul4_bh <= C4_16;\n    end\n    3'd2:\n    begin\n        i0     <= d0 - d4;\n    end\n    3'd3:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= d0; mul0_bl <= C7_16; mul0_bh <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= d6; mul1_bl <= C1_16; mul1_bh <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= d4; mul2_bl <= C3_16; mul2_bh <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= d2; mul3_bl <= C5_16; mul3_bh <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd4:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= d0; mul0_bl <= C7_16; mul0_bh <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= d6; mul1_bl <= C1_16; mul1_bh <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= d4; mul2_bl <= C3_16; mul2_bh <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= d2; mul3_bl <= C5_16; mul3_bh <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd5:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= d2; mul0_bl <= C6_16; mul0_bh <= C6_16;\n        mul1_a <= d6; mul1_bl <= C2_16; mul1_bh <= C2_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0; mul4_bl <= C4_16; mul4_bh <= C4_16;\n    end\n    default:\n        ;\n    endcase\n    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */\nend\n\n// 2-stage split of each product a*b (a = 16/17-bit signed, b = 13-bit const):\n// stage 1 registers lo = a[7:0]*b and hi = a[15:8]*b (hi signed);\n// stage 2 forms lo + (hi << 8) = a*b exactly (no wrap at these widths).\nwire signed [20:0] p0lo = $signed({1'b0, mul0_a[7:0]}) * mul0_bl;\nwire signed [20:0] p0hi = $signed(mul0_a[15:8]) * mul0_bh;\nwire signed [20:0] p1lo = $signed({1'b0, mul1_a[7:0]}) * mul1_bl;\nwire signed [20:0] p1hi = $signed(mul1_a[15:8]) * mul1_bh;\nwire signed [20:0] p2lo = $signed({1'b0, mul2_a[7:0]}) * mul2_bl;\nwire signed [20:0] p2hi = $signed(mul2_a[15:8]) * mul2_bh;\nwire signed [20:0] p3lo = $signed({1'b0, mul3_a[7:0]}) * mul3_bl;\nwire signed [20:0] p3hi = $signed(mul3_a[15:8]) * mul3_bh;\nwire signed [20:0] p4lo = $signed({1'b0, mul4_a[7:0]}) * mul4_bl;\nwire signed [20:0] p4hi = $signed(mul4_a[16:8]) * mul4_bh;\n\nreg signed [20:0] lo0_q, lo1_q, lo2_q, lo3_q, lo4_q;\nreg signed [20:0] hi0_q, hi1_q, hi2_q, hi3_q, hi4_q;\n\nreg signed [27:0] mul0, mul1, mul2, mul3;\nreg signed [28:0] mul4;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    lo0_q <= 0; lo1_q <= 0; lo2_q <= 0; lo3_q <= 0; lo4_q <= 0;\n    hi0_q <= 0; hi1_q <= 0; hi2_q <= 0; hi3_q <= 0; hi4_q <= 0;\n    mul0 <= 0; mul1 <= 0; mul2 <= 0; mul3 <= 0; mul4 <= 0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    lo0_q <= p0lo; hi0_q <= p0hi;\n    lo1_q <= p1lo; hi1_q <= p1hi;\n    lo2_q <= p2lo; hi2_q <= p2hi;\n    lo3_q <= p3lo; hi3_q <= p3hi;\n    lo4_q <= p4lo; hi4_q <= p4hi;\n    mul0 <= lo0_q + (hi0_q <<< 8);\n    mul1 <= lo1_q + (hi1_q <<< 8);\n    mul2 <= lo2_q + (hi2_q <<< 8);\n    mul3 <= lo3_q + (hi3_q <<< 8);\n    mul4 <= lo4_q + (hi4_q <<< 8);\nend\n\nreg        out_stg0_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg1_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg2_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg0_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg1_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg2_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i; out_stg0_idx_q <= inport_idx_i;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q; out_stg1_idx_q <= out_stg0_idx_q;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q; out_stg2_idx_q <= out_stg1_idx_q;\nend\n\n// all x-pass internals bounded by 570.7M < 2^30 -> 31-bit signed\nreg signed [30:0] o_s5, o_s6, o_s7;\nreg signed [30:0] o_t0, o_t1, o_t2, o_t3, o_t4, o_t5, o_t6, o_t7;\nreg signed [30:0] o_t6_5, o_t5_6;\n\n//--- 1/sqrt(2) scaling: d = trunc((x*181 mod 2^32)/256) (signed, exact) ------\n// p = (x*181) mod 2^32 ; d = (p >>> 8) + (p[31] && p[7:0]!=0)\n// p[7:0] = (x[7:0]*181) mod 256 (181 odd) -> zero detect computed early.\nwire signed [31:0] px5 = o_t6_5 * 181;\nwire signed [31:0] px6 = o_t5_6 * 181;\nwire        nzx5 = ((o_t6_5[7:0] * 8'd181) != 8'd0);\nwire        nzx6 = ((o_t5_6[7:0] * 8'd181) != 8'd0);\n\nreg signed [23:0] s5hi_q, s6hi_q;   // p >>> 8 (fits 24-bit signed)\nreg signed [31:0] f5_q, f6_q;       // truncation fixup (0 or +1), signed\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    s5hi_q <= 24'b0; s6hi_q <= 24'b0; f5_q <= 32'b0; f6_q <= 32'b0;\nend\nelse if (out_stg2_idx_q == 3'd7)\nbegin\n    s5hi_q <= px5[31:8];\n    s6hi_q <= px6[31:8];\n    f5_q   <= px5[31] & nzx5;\n    f6_q   <= px6[31] & nzx6;\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    o_s5 <= 0; o_s6 <= 0; o_s7 <= 0; o_t0 <= 0; o_t1 <= 0; o_t2 <= 0;\n    o_t3 <= 0; o_t4 <= 0; o_t5 <= 0; o_t6 <= 0; o_t7 <= 0;\n    o_t6_5 <= 0; o_t5_6 <= 0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    case (out_stg2_idx_q)\n    3'd0: begin o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; end\n    3'd1: begin o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1; o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3; o_t0 <= mul4; end\n    3'd2: begin o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7; end\n    3'd3: begin o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3); end\n    3'd4: begin o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3; end\n    3'd5: begin o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; o_t4 <= mul4; o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5; o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6; end\n    3'd6: begin o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3; o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3; o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5; o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6; end\n    default: ;\n    endcase\nend\n\nreg        out_stg3_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg3_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q; out_stg3_idx_q <= out_stg2_idx_q;\nend\n\n// |out| <= 346243 < 2^19 -> 20-bit exact\nreg signed [19:0] block_out [0:7];\nreg signed [19:0] block_out_tmp;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_out[0] <= 0; block_out[1] <= 0; block_out[2] <= 0; block_out[3] <= 0;\n    block_out[4] <= 0; block_out[5] <= 0; block_out[6] <= 0; block_out[7] <= 0;\n    block_out_tmp <= 0;\nend\nelse if (out_stg3_valid_q)\nbegin\n    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)\n    begin\n        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)\n        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)\n    begin\n        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + s5hi_q + f5_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - s5hi_q - f5_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + s6hi_q + f6_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - s6hi_q - f6_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */\nend\n\nreg [7:0] valid_q;\nreg [5:0] ptr_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse\n    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (outport_valid_o)\n    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;\n\nassign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];\nassign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];\nassign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Double-buffered corner turn (4 lanes x 2 halves x 16 entries)\n// Read path: one-hot decode + AND-OR tree (low fanout, shallow).\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jt_ram\n#(\n     parameter W = 20\n)\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           wr_en\n    ,input           block_wr\n    ,input  [  3:0]  wr_addr\n    ,input  [W-1:0]  wr_data\n    ,input           block_rd\n    ,input  [  3:0]  rd_addr\n    ,output reg [W-1:0]  rd_data\n);\n\nreg [W-1:0] mem0 [0:15];\nreg [W-1:0] mem1 [0:15];\n\n// one-hot decode of the read address\nreg [15:0] oh;\ninteger i;\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    for (i = 0; i < 16; i = i + 1)\n        oh[i] = (rd_addr == i);\nend\n\nwire [15:0] oh0 = oh & {16{~block_rd}};\nwire [15:0] oh1 = oh & {16{ block_rd}};\n\n// gated words: exactly one of the 32 is (possibly) non-zero\nreg [W-1:0] gt [0:31];\nreg [W-1:0] t1 [0:15];\nreg [W-1:0] t2 [0:7];\nreg [W-1:0] t3 [0:3];\nreg [W-1:0] t4 [0:1];\nreg [W-1:0] acc;\ninteger k;\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1)\n    begin\n        gt[k]    = mem0[k] & {W{oh0[k]}};\n        gt[k+16] = mem1[k] & {W{oh1[k]}};\n    end\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1) t1[k] = gt[2*k] | gt[2*k+1];\n    for (k = 0; k < 8;  k = k + 1) t2[k] = t1[2*k] | t1[2*k+1];\n    for (k = 0; k < 4;  k = k + 1) t3[k] = t2[2*k] | t2[2*k+1];\n    for (k = 0; k < 2;  k = k + 1) t4[k] = t3[2*k] | t3[2*k+1];\n    acc = t4[0] | t4[1];\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (wr_en)\n    begin\n        if (block_wr)\n            mem1[wr_addr] <= wr_data;\n        else\n            mem0[wr_addr] <= wr_data;\n    end\n\n    rd_data <= acc;\nend\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule jpeg_idct_transpose\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data_i\n    ,input  [  5:0]  inport_idx_i\n    ,input           outport_ready_i\n    ,output          inport_accept_o\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data0_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data1_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data2_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data3_o\n    ,output [  2:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\nreg         block_wr_q;\nreg         block_rd_q;\nreg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;\nreg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;\nreg [3:0]   rda0_q, rda1_q, rda2_q, rda3_q;\n\nwire [3:0]  wr_addr_w = inport_idx_i[3:0];\n\nwire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);\nwire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);\nwire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);\nwire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram0\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr0_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda0_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data0_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram1\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr1_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda1_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data1_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram2\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr2_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda2_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data2_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram3\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr3_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda3_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data3_o)\n);\n\nreg [1:0] block_ready_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q    <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q    <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse if (img_start_i)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q    <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q    <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;\n        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;\n    end\n\n    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;\n        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;\n    end\nend\n\nassign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];\n\nlocalparam STATE_W           = 2;\nlocalparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;\nlocalparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;\nlocalparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;\n\nreg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;\nreg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;\n\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    next_state_r = state_q;\n    case (state_q)\n    STATE_IDLE:\n        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)\n            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;\n    STATE_SETUP:\n        next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;\n    STATE_ACTIVE:\n        if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63)\n            next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\n    default: ;\n    endcase\n    if (img_start_i)\n        next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    state_q ,.outport_data0_o(transpose_data0_w)\n    ,.outport_data1_o(transpose_data1_w)\n    ,.outport_data2_o(transpose_data2_w)\n    ,.outport_data3_o(transpose_data3_w)\n    ,.outport_idx_o(transpose_idx_w)\n    ,.outport_ready_i(1'b1)\n);\n\njpeg_idct_y\nu_idct_y\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.img_start_i(img_start_i)\n    ,.img_end_i(img_end_i)\n ,.inport_data3_i(transpose_data3_w)\n    ,.inport_idx_i(transpose_idx_w)\n    ,.outport_valid_o(outport_valid_o)\n    ,.outport_data_o(outport_data_o)\n    ,.outport_idx_o(outport_idx_o)\n);\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// 1-D IDCT, row pass (input coefficients 16-bit signed)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_x\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data0_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data1_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data2_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0] signed [12:0] mul4_bl, mul4_bm, mul4_bh;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    i0 <= 17'b0;\n    mul0_a <= 16'b0; mul0_bl <= 0; mul0_bm <= 0; mul0_bh <= 0;\n    mul1_a <= 16'b0; mul1_bl <= d2; mul3_bl <= C3_16; mul3_bm <= C3_16; mul3_bh <= C3_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0; mul4_bl <= C4_16; mul4_bm <= C4_16; mul4_bh <= C4_16;\n    end\n    3'd2:\n    begin\n        i0     <= d0 - d4;\n      mul3_a <= d2; mul3_bl <= C5_16; mul3_bm <= C5_16; mul3_bh <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd5:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= d2; mul0_bl <= C6_16; mul0_bm <= C6_16; mul0_bh <= C6_16;\n        mul1_a <= lint_on WIDTH */\nend\n\n// 3-way split of each product a*b (exact; |a*b| < 2^28 for all inputs):\n// a = a[6:0] + (a[13:7] << 7) + (a[15:14] << 14)\n// stage 1 registers the three partial products; $signed(mul2_a[15:14]) * mul2_bh;\nwire signed [19:0] p3lo = $signed({1'b0, mul3_a[6:0]}) * mul3_bl;\nwire signed [19:0] p3mi = $signed({1'b0, mul3_a[13:7]}) * mul3_bm;\nwire signed [19:0] p3hi = $signed(mul3_a[15:14]) * mul3_bh;\nwire signed [19:0] p4lo = $signed({1'b0, mul4_a[6:0]}) * mul4_bl;\nwire signed [19:0] p4mi = $signed({1'b0, mul4_a[13:7]}) * mul4_bm;\nwire signed [19:0] p4hi = $signed(mul4_a[16:14]) * mul4_bh;\n\nreg signed [19:0] lo0_q, mi0_q, hi0_q;\nreg signed [19:0] lo1_q, mi1_q, hi1_q;\nreg signed [19:0] lo2_q, mi2_q, hi2_q;\nreg signed [19:0] lo3_q, mi3_q, hi3_q;\nreg out_stg0_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg1_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg2_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg0_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg1_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg2_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i; out_stg0_idx_q <= inport_idx_i;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q; out_stg1_idx_q <= out_stg0_idx_q;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q; o_t6, o_t7;\nreg signed [30:0] o_t6_5, o_t5_6;\n\n//--- 1/sqrt(2) scaling: d = trunc((x*181 mod 2^32)/256) (signed, exact) ------\n// p = (x*181) mod 2^32 ; d = (p >>> 8) + (p[31] && p[7:0]!=0)\n// 8'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse\n    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (outport_valid_o)\n    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;\n\nassign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];\nassign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];\nassign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Double-buffered corner turn (4 lanes x 2 halves x 16 entries)\n// Read path: one-hot decode + AND-OR tree (low fanout, shallow).\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jt_ram\n#(\n     parameter W = 20\n)\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           wr_en\n    ,input           block_wr\n    ,input  [  3:0]  wr_addr\n    ,input  [W-1:0]  wr_data\n    ,input           block_rd\n    ,input  [  3:0]  rd_addr\n    ,output reg [W-1:0]  rd_data\n);\n\nreg [W-1:0] mem0 [0:15];\nreg [W-1:0] mem1 [0:15];\n\n// one-hot decode of the read address\nreg [15:0] oh;\ninteger i;\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    for (i = 0; i < 16; i = i + 1)\n        oh[i] = (rd_addr == i);\nend\n\nwire [15:0] oh0 = oh & {16{~block_rd}};\nwire [15:0] oh1 = oh & {16{ block_rd}};\n\n// gated words: exactly one of the 32 is (possibly) non-zero\nreg [W-1:0] gt [0:31];\nreg [W-1:0] t1 [0:15];\nreg [W-1:0] t2 [0:7];\nreg [W-1:0] t3 [0:3];\nreg [W-1:0] t4 [0:1];\nreg [W-1:0] acc;\ninteger k;\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1)\n    begin\n        gt[k]    = mem0[k] & {W{oh0[k]}};\n        gt[k+16] = mem1[k] & {W{oh1[k]}};\n    end\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1) t1[k] = gt[2*k] | gt[2*k+1];\n    for (k = 0; k < 8;  k = k + 1) t2[k] = t1[2*k] | t1[2*k+1];\n (wr_en)\n    begin\n        if (block_wr)\n            mem1[wr_addr] <= wr_data;\n        else\n            mem0[wr_addr] <= wr_data;\n    end\n\n    rd_data <= acc;\nend\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule jpeg_idct_transpose\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0] block_wr_q;\nreg         block_rd_q;\nreg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;\nreg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;\nreg [3:0]   rda0_q, rda1_q, rda2_q, rda3_q;\n\nwire [3:0]  wr_addr_w = inport_idx_i[3:0];\n\nwire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);\nwire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);\nwire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);\nwire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram0\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr0_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda0_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data0_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram1\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr1_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda1_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data1_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram2\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr2_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda2_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data2_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram3\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr3_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n (rst)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q    <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q    <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse if (img_start_i)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q    <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q    <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;\n        block_wr_q [ASSISTANT]
v8 banked at **4.16M**. The target_delay knob has no effect on the netlist (identical results at 250 ps), so RTL structure is the only lever. The stage-1 critical path is the 3-operand recombine CPA sitting with the arrays. v9: split that CPA across the two existing stages — stage 1 = partial arrays + **carry-save (3:2) combine** registered as sum/carry, stage 2 = final 2-operand CPA. Same pipeline depth (schedule untouched), exact via mod-2^N identities:

[30:0] o_t0, o_t1, o_t2, o_t3, o_t4, o_t5, o_t6, o_t7;\nreg signed [30:0] o_t6_5, o_t5_6;\n\n//--- 1/sqrt(2) scaling: d = trunc((x*181 mod 2^32)/256) (signed, exact) ------\n// p = (x*181) mod 2^32 ; d = (p >>> 8) + (p[31] && p[7:0]!=0)\n// p[7:0] = (x[7:0]*181) mod 256 (181 odd) -> zero detect computed early.\nwire signed [31:0] px5 = o_t6_5 * 181;\nwire signed [31:0] px6 = o_t5_6 * 181;\nwire        nzx5 = ((o_t6_5[7:0] * 8'd181) != 8'd0);\nwire        nzx6 = ((o_t5_6[7:0] * 8'd181) != 8'd0);\n\nreg signed [23:0] s5hi_q, s6hi_q;   // p >>> 8 (fits 24-bit signed)\nreg signed [31:0] f5_q, f6_q;       // truncation fixup (0 or +1), signed\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    s5hi_q <= 24'b0; s6hi_q <= 24'b0; f5_q <= 32'b0; f6_q <= 32'b0;\nend\nelse if (out_stg2_idx_q == 3'd7)\nbegin\n    s5hi_q <= px5[31:8];\n    s6hi_q <= px6[31:8];\n    f5_q   <= px5[31] & nzx5;\n    f6_q   <= px6[31] & nzx6;\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    o_s5 <= 0; o_s6 <= 0; o_s7 <= 0; o_t0 <= 0; o_t1 <= 0; o_t2 <= 0;\n    o_t3 <= 0; o_t4 <= 0; o_t5 <= 0; o_t6 <= 0; o_t7 <= 0;\n    o_t6_5 <= 0; o_t5_6 <= 0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    case (out_stg2_idx_q)\n    3'd0: begin o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; end\n    3'd1: begin o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1; o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3; o_t0 <= mul4; end\n    3'd2: begin o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7; end\n    3'd3: begin o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3); end\n    3'd4: begin o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3; end\n    3'd5: begin o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; o_t4 <= mul4; o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5; o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6; end\n    3'd6: begin o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3; o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3; o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5; o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6; end\n    default: ;\n    endcase\nend\n\nreg        out_stg3_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg3_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q; out_stg3_idx_q <= out_stg2_idx_q;\nend\n\n// |out| <= 346243 < 2^19 -> 20-bit exact\nreg signed [19:0] block_out [0:7];\nreg signed [19:0] block_out_tmp;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_out[0] <= 0; block_out[1] <= 0; block_out[2] <= 0; block_out[3] <= 0;\n    block_out[4] <= 0; block_out[5] <= 0; block_out[6] <= 0; block_out[7] <= 0;\n    block_out_tmp <= 0;\nend\nelse if (out_stg3_valid_q)\nbegin\n    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)\n    begin\n        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)\n        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)\n    begin\n        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + s5hi_q + f5_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - s5hi_q - f5_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + s6hi_q + f6_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - s6hi_q - f6_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */\nend\n\nreg [7:0] valid_q;\nreg [5:0] ptr_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse\n    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (outport_valid_o)\n    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;\n\nassign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];\nassign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];\nassign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Double-buffered corner turn (4 lanes x 2 halves x 16 entries)\n// Read path: one-hot decode + AND-OR tree (low fanout, shallow).\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jt_ram\n#(\n     parameter W = 20\n)\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           wr_en\n    ,input           block_wr\n    ,input  [  3:0]  wr_addr\n    ,input  [W-1:0]  wr_data\n    ,input           block_rd\n    ,input  [  3:0]  rd_addr\n    ,output reg [W-1:0]  rd_data\n);\n\nreg [W-1:0] mem0 [0:15];\nreg [W-1:0] mem1 [0:15];\n\n// one-hot decode of the read address\nreg [15:0] oh;\ninteger i;\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    for (i = 0; i < 16; i = i + 1)\n        oh[i] = (rd_addr == i);\nend\n\nwire [15:0] oh0 = oh & {16{~block_rd}};\nwire [15:0] oh1 = oh & {16{ block_rd}};\n\n// gated words: exactly one of the 32 is (possibly) non-zero\nreg [W-1:0] gt [0:31];\nreg [W-1:0] t1 [0:15];\nreg [W-1:0] t2 [0:7];\nreg [W-1:0] t3 [0:3];\nreg [W-1:0] t4 [0:1];\nreg [W-1:0] acc;\ninteger k;\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1)\n    begin\n        gt[k]    = mem0[k] & {W{oh0[k]}};\n        gt[k+16] = mem1[k] & {W{oh1[k]}};\n    end\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1) t1[k] = gt[2*k] | gt[2*k+1];\n    for (k = 0; k < 8;  k = k + 1) t2[k] = t1[2*k] | t1[2*k+1];\n    for (k = 0; k < 4;  k = k + 1) t3[k] = t2[2*k] | t2[2*k+1];\n    for (k = 0; k < 2;  k = k + 1) t4[k] = t3[2*k] | t3[2*k+1];\n    acc = t4[0] | t4[1];\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (wr_en)\n    begin\n        if (block_wr)\n            mem1[wr_addr] <= wr_data;\n        else\n            mem0[wr_addr] <= wr_data;\n    end\n\n    rd_data <= acc;\nend\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule jpeg_idct_transpose\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data_i\n    ,input  [  5:0]  inport_idx_i\n    ,input           outport_ready_i\n    ,output          inport_accept_o\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data0_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data1_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data2_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data3_o\n    ,output [  2:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\nreg         block_wr_q;\nreg         block_rd_q;\nreg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;\nreg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;\nreg [3:0]   rda0_q, rda1_q, rda2_q, rda3_q;\n\nwire [3:0]  wr_addr_w = inport_idx_i[3:0];\n\nwire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);\nwire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);\nwire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);\nwire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram0\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr0_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda0_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data0_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram1\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr1_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda1_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data1_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram2\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr2_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda2_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data2_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram3\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr3_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda3_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data3_o)\n);\n\nreg [1:0] block_ready_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q    <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q    <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse if (img_start_i)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q    <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q    <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;\n        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;\n    end\n\n    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;\n        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;\n    end\nend\n\nassign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];\n\nlocalparam STATE_W           = 2;\nlocalparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;\nlocalparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;\nlocalparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;\n\nreg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;\nreg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;\n\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    next_state_r = state_q;\n    case (state_q)\n    STATE_IDLE:\n        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)\n            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;\n    STATE_SETUP:\n        next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;\n    STATE_ACTIVE:\n        if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63)\n            next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\n    default: ;\n    endcase\n    if (img_start_i)\n        next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    state_q <= STATE_IDLE;\nelse\n    state_q <= next_state_r;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;\nelse if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)\n    rd_idx_q <= rd_idx_q + 6'd1;\n\n// read address (identical schedule to original), replicated per RAM to\n// cut select-net fanout\nreg [3:0] rda_next;\n\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    case (state_q)\n    STATE_IDLE:\n        rda_next = 4'd0;\n    STATE_SETUP:\n        rda_next = 4'd8;\n    STATE_ACTIVE:\n    begin\n        case (rd_idx_q[2:0])\n        3'd0: rda_next = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd1: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        3'd2: rda_next = rd_addr_q;\n        3'd3: rda_next = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd4: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        3'd5: rda_next = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd6: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd1;\n        3'd7: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        endcase\n    end\n    default:\n        rda_next = rd_addr_q;\n    endcase\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda0_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda1_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda2_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda3_q <= 4'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    rd_addr_q <= rda_next;\n    rda0_q <= rda_next;\n    rda1_q <= rda_next;\n    rda2_q <= rda_next;\n    rda3_q <= rda_next;\nend\n\nassign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);\nassign outport_idx_o   = rd_idx_q[2:0];\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// 1-D IDCT, column pass (inputs are the 20-bit x-pass results)\n// products exact mod 2^32 (identical to the original 32-bit multiply)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_y\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data0_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data1_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data2_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data3_i\n    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o\n    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\nlocalparam OUT_SHIFT = 15;\n\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C1_16 = 13'sd4017;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C2_16 = 13'sd3784;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C3_16 = 13'sd3406;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C4_16 = 13'sd2896;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C5_16 = 13'sd2276;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C6_16 = 13'sd1567;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C7_16 = 13'sd799;\n\nwire signed [19:0] block_in_0_1 = inport_data0_i;\nwire signed [19:0] block_in_2_3 = inport_data1_i;\nwire signed [19:0] block_in_4_5 = inport_data2_i;\nwire signed [19:0] block_in_6_7 = inport_data3_i;\n\nreg signed [20:0] i0;\nreg signed [19:0] mul0_a, mul1_a, mul2_a, mul3_a;\nreg signed [20:0] mul4_a;\nreg signed [12:0] mul0_bl, mul0_bm, mul0_bh;\nreg signed [12:0] mul1_bl, mul1_bm, mul1_bh;\nreg signed [12:0] mul2_bl, mul2_bm, mul2_bh;\nreg signed [12:0] mul3_bl, mul3_bm, mul3_bh;\nreg signed [12:0] mul4_bl, mul4_bm, mul4_bh;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    i0 <= 21'b0;\n    mul0_a <= 20'b0; mul0_bl <= 0; mul0_bm <= 0; mul0_bh <= 0;\n    mul1_a <= 20'b0; mul1_bl <= 0; mul1_bm <= 0; mul1_bh <= 0;\n    mul2_a <= 20'b0; mul2_bl <= 0; mul2_bm <= 0; mul2_bh <= 0;\n    mul3_a <= 20'b0; mul3_bl <= 0; mul3_bm <= 0; mul3_bh <= 0;\n    mul4_a <= 21'b0; mul4_bl <= 0; mul4_bm <= 0; mul4_bh <= 0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */\n    case (inport_idx_i)\n    3'd0:\n    begin\n        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; mul0_bl <= C2_16; mul0_bm <= C2_16; mul0_bh <= C2_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_bl <= C6_16; mul1_bm <= C6_16; mul1_bh <= C6_16;\n    end\n    3'd1:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_bl <= C1_16; mul0_bm <= C1_16; mul0_bh <= C1_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_bl <= C7_16; mul1_bm <= C7_16; mul1_bh <= C7_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_bl <= C5_16; mul2_bm <= C5_16; mul2_bh <= C5_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_bl <= C3_16; mul3_bm <= C3_16; mul3_bh <= C3_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0;           mul4_bl <= C4_16; mul4_bm <= C4_16; mul4_bh <= C4_16;\n    end\n    3'd2:\n    begin\n        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;\n    end\n    3'd3:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_bl <= C7_16; mul0_bm <= C7_16; mul0_bh <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_bl <= C1_16; mul1_bm <= C1_16; mul1_bh <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_bl <= C3_16; mul2_bm <= C3_16; mul2_bh <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_bl <= C5_16; mul3_bm <= C5_16; mul3_bh <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd4:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; mul0_bl <= C7_16; mul0_bm <= C7_16; mul0_bh <= C7_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_bl <= C1_16; mul1_bm <= C1_16; mul1_bh <= C1_16;\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; mul2_bl <= C3_16; mul2_bm <= C3_16; mul2_bh <= C3_16;\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; mul3_bl <= C5_16; mul3_bm <= C5_16; mul3_bh <= C5_16;\n    end\n    3'd5:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; mul0_bl <= C6_16; mul0_bm <= C6_16; mul0_bh <= C6_16;\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; mul1_bl <= C2_16; mul1_bm <= C2_16; mul1_bh <= C2_16;\n        mul4_a <= i0;           mul4_bl <= C4_16; mul4_bm <= C4_16; mul4_bh <= C4_16;\n    end\n    default:\n        ;\n    endcase\n    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */\nend\n\n// 3-way split of each product a*b, exact mod 2^32 (as the original):\n// a = a[6:0] + (a[13:7] << 7) + (a[19:14] << 14)   (mul4: a[20:14])\nwire signed [19:0] p0lo = $signed({1'b0, mul0_a[6:0]}) * mul0_bl;\nwire signed [19:0] p0mi = $signed({1'b0, mul0_a[13:7]}) * mul0_bm;\nwire signed [17:0] p0hi = $signed(mul0_a[19:14]) * mul0_bh;\nwire signed [19:0] p1lo = $signed({1'b0, mul1_a[6:0]}) * mul1_bl;\nwire signed [19:0] p1mi = $signed({1'b0, mul1_a[13:7]}) * mul1_bm;\nwire signed [17:0] p1hi = $signed(mul1_a[19:14]) * mul1_bh;\nwire signed [19:0] p2lo = $signed({1'b0, mul2_a[6:0]}) * mul2_bl;\nwire signed [19:0] p2mi = $signed({1'b0, mul2_a[13:7]}) * mul2_bm;\nwire signed [17:0] p2hi = $signed(mul2_a[19:14]) * mul2_bh;\nwire signed [19:0] p3lo = $signed({1'b0, mul3_a[6:0]}) * mul3_bl;\nwire signed [19:0] p3mi = $signed({1'b0, mul3_a[13:7]}) * mul3_bm;\nwire signed [17:0] p3hi = $signed(mul3_a[19:14]) * mul3_bh;\nwire signed [19:0] p4lo = $signed({1'b0, mul4_a[6:0]}) * mul4_bl;\nwire signed [19:0] p4mi = $signed({1'b0, mul4_a[13:7]}) * mul4_bm;\nwire signed [17:0] p4hi = $signed(mul4_a[20:14]) * mul4_bh;\n\nwire signed [31:0] ya0 = {{12{p0lo[19]}}, p0lo};\nwire signed [31:0] yb0 = {{5{p0mi[19]}}, p0mi, 7'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] yc0 = {p0hi, 14'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] ya1 = {{12{p1lo[19]}}, p1lo};\nwire signed [31:0] yb1 = {{5{p1mi[19]}}, p1mi, 7'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] yc1 = {p1hi, 14'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] ya2 = {{12{p2lo[19]}}, p2lo};\nwire signed [31:0] yb2 = {{5{p2mi[19]}}, p2mi, 7'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] yc2 = {p2hi, 14'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] ya3 = {{12{p3lo[19]}}, p3lo};\nwire signed [31:0] yb3 = {{5{p3mi[19]}}, p3mi, 7'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] yc3 = {p3hi, 14'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] ya4 = {{12{p4lo[19]}}, p4lo};\nwire signed [31:0] yb4 = {{5{p4mi[19]}}, p4mi, 7'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] yc4 = {p4hi, 14'b0};\n\nwire [31:0] ys0 = ya0 ^ yb0 ^ yc0;\nwire [31:0] ycarr0 = (ya0 & yb0) | (ya0 & yc0) | (yb0 & yc0);\nwire [31:0] ys1 = ya1 ^ yb1 ^ yc1;\nwire [31:0] ycarr1 = (ya1 & yb1) | (ya1 & yc1) | (yb1 & yc1);\nwire [31:0] ys2 = ya2 ^ yb2 ^ yc2;\nwire [31:0] ycarr2 = (ya2 & yb2) | (ya2 & yc2) | (yb2 & yc2);\nwire [31:0] ys3 = ya3 ^ yb3 ^ yc3;\nwire [31:0] ycarr3 = (ya3 & yb3) | (ya3 & yc3) | (yb3 & yc3);\nwire [31:0] ys4 = ya4 ^ yb4 ^ yc4;\nwire [31:0] ycarr4 = (ya4 & yb4) | (ya4 & yc4) | (yb4 & yc4);\n\nreg signed [31:0] s0_q, c0_q, s1_q, c1_q, s2_q, c2_q, s3_q, c3_q, s4_q, [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg0_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg1_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= [31:0] o_t6_5, o_t5_6;\n\n//--- 1/sqrt(2) scaling: d = trunc((x*181 mod 2^32)/256) (signed, exact) ------\n// p = (x*181) mod 2^32 ; d = (p >>> 8) + (p[31] && p[7:0]!=0)\n// p[7:0] = (x[7:0]*181) mod mul3; o_t0 <= mul4; end\n    3'd2: begin o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7; end\n    3'd3: begin o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3); end\n    3'd4: begin o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3; end\n    3'd5: begin o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; o_t4 <= mul4; o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5; o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6; end\n    3'd6: begin o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3; o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3; o_t6_5 <= == 3'd3)\n    begin\n        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)\n        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)\n    begin\n        block_out[2] [ASSISTANT]
Synthesis is insensitive to `target_delay` (500/250/1200 all ≈ same netlist). Area-cut idea with delay upside: in v9's carry-save structure, the **stage-2 final CPA is redundant** — consumers can combine the (sum,carry) pair directly (one fewer 30-bit adder per multiplier, 10 total, plus fewer registers). The consumer expressions become carry-save 3-operand adds. Schedule unchanged; exact via mod-2ᴺ identities. Writing v10:

tree (low fanout, shallow).\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jt_ram\n#(\n     parameter W = 20\n)\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           wr_en\n [W-1:0] mem0 [0:15];\nreg [W-1:0] mem1 [0:15];\n\n// one-hot decode of the read address\nreg [15:0] oh;\ninteger i;\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    for (i = 0; i < 16; i = i + 1)\n        oh[i] = (rd_addr == ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data0_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data1_i\n
