5:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\nwire          idct_x_valid_w;\nwire [ 19:0]  idct_x_data_w;\nwire [  5:0]  idct_x_idx_w;\n\njpeg_idct_x\nu_idct_x\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.img_start_i(img_start_i)\n    ,.img_end_i(img_end_i)\n    ,.inport_valid_i(inport_valid_i)\n    ,.inport_data0_i(inport_data0_i)\n    ,.inport_data1_i(inport_data1_i)\n    ,.inport_data2_i(inport_data2_i)\n    ,.inport_data3_i(inport_data3_i)\n    ,.inport_idx_i(inport_idx_i)\n    ,.outport_valid_o(idct_x_valid_w)\n    ,.outport_data_o(idct_x_data_w)\n    ,.outport_idx_o(idct_x_idx_w)\n);\n\nwire          transpose_valid_w;\nwire [ 19:0]  transpose_data0_w;\nwire [ 19:0]  transpose_data1_w;\nwire [ 19:0]  transpose_data2_w;\nwire [ 19:0]  transpose_data3_w;\nwire [  2:0]  transpose_idx_w;\n\njpeg_idct_transpose\nu_transpose\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.img_start_i(img_start_i)\n    ,.img_end_i(img_end_i)\n    ,.inport_valid_i(idct_x_valid_w)\n    ,.inport_data_i(idct_x_data_w)\n    ,.inport_idx_i(idct_x_idx_w)\n    ,.inport_accept_o()\n    ,.outport_valid_o(transpose_valid_w)\n    ,.outport_data0_o(transpose_data0_w)\n    ,.outport_data1_o(transpose_data1_w)\n    ,.outport_data2_o(transpose_data2_w)\n    ,.outport_data3_o(transpose_data3_w)\n    ,.outport_idx_o(transpose_idx_w)\n    ,.outport_ready_i(1'b1)\n);\n\njpeg_idct_y\nu_idct_y\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.rst(rst)\n    ,.img_start_i(img_start_i)\n    ,.img_end_i(img_end_i)\n    ,.inport_valid_i(transpose_valid_w)\n    ,.inport_data0_i(transpose_data0_w)\n    ,.inport_data1_i(transpose_data1_w)\n    ,.inport_data2_i(transpose_data2_w)\n    ,.inport_data3_i(transpose_data3_w)\n    ,.inport_idx_i(transpose_idx_w)\n    ,.outport_valid_o(outport_valid_o)\n    ,.outport_data_o(outport_data_o)\n    ,.outport_idx_o(outport_idx_o)\n);\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// 1-D IDCT, row pass (input coefficients 16-bit signed)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_x\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data0_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data1_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data2_i\n    ,input  [ 15:0]  inport_data3_i\n    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data_o\n    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\nlocalparam OUT_SHIFT = 11;\n\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C1_16 = 13'sd4017;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C2_16 = 13'sd3784;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C3_16 = 13'sd3406;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C4_16 = 13'sd2896;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C5_16 = 13'sd2276;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C6_16 = 13'sd1567;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C7_16 = 13'sd799;\n\nwire signed [15:0] d0 = inport_data0_i;\nwire signed [15:0] d2 = inport_data1_i;\nwire signed [15:0] d4 = inport_data2_i;\nwire signed [15:0] d6 = inport_data3_i;\n\nreg signed [16:0] i0;\nreg signed [15:0] mul0_a, mul1_a, mul2_a, mul3_a;\nreg signed [16:0] mul4_a;\nreg signed [12:0] mul0_b0, mul0_b1, mul0_b2, mul0_b3;\nreg signed [12:0] mul1_b0, mul1_b1, mul1_b2, mul1_b3;\nreg signed [12:0] mul2_b0, mul2_b1, mul2_b2, mul2_b3;\nreg signed [12:0] mul3_b0, mul3_b1, mul3_b2, mul3_b3;\nreg signed [12:0] mul4_b0, mul4_b1, mul4_b2, mul4_b3;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    i0 <= 17'b0;\n    mul0_a <= 16'b0; {mul0_b0, mul0_b1, mul0_b2, mul0_b3} <= 0;\n    mul1_a <= 16'b0; {mul1_b0, mul1_b1, mul1_b2, mul1_b3} <= 0;\n    mul2_a <= 16'b0; {mul2_b0, mul2_b1, mul2_b2, mul2_b3} <= 0;\n    mul3_a <= 16'b0; {mul3_b0, mul3_b1, mul3_b2, mul3_b3} <= 0;\n    mul4_a <= 17'b0; {mul4_b0, mul4_b1, mul4_b2, mul4_b3} <= 0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */\n    case (inport_idx_i)\n    3'd0:\n    begin\n        i0     <= d0 + d4;\n        mul0_a <= d2; {mul0_b0, mul0_b1, mul0_b2, mul0_b3} <= {C2_16,C2_16,C2_16,C2_16};\n        mul1_a <= d6; {mul1_b0, mul1_b1, mul1_b2, mul1_b3} <= {C6_16,C6_16,C6_16,C6_16};\n    end\n    3'd1:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= d0; {mul0_b0, mul0_b1, mul0_b2, mul0_b3} <= {C1_16,C1_16,C1_16,C1_16};\n        mul1_a <= d6; {mul1_b0, mul1_b1, mul1_b2, mul1_b3} <= {C7_16,C7_16,C7_16,C7_16};\n        mul2_a <= d4; {mul2_b0, mul2_b1, mul2_b2, mul2_b3} <= {C5_16,C5_16,C5_16,C5_16};\n        mul3_a <= d2; {mul3_b0, mul3_b1, mul3_b2, mul3_b3} <= {C3_16,C3_16,C3_16,C3_16};\n        mul4_a <= i0; {mul4_b0, mul4_b1, mul4_b2, mul4_b3} <= {C4_16,C4_16,C4_16,C4_16};\n    end\n    3'd2:\n    begin\n        i0     <= d0 - d4;\n    end\n    3'd3:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= d0; {mul0_b0, mul0_b1, mul0_b2, mul0_b3} <= {C7_16,C7_16,C7_16,C7_16};\n        mul1_a <= d6; {mul1_b0, mul1_b1, mul1_b2, mul1_b3} <= {C1_16,C1_16,C1_16,C1_16};\n        mul2_a <= d4; {mul2_b0, mul2_b1, mul2_b2, mul2_b3} <= {C3_16,C3_16,C3_16,C3_16};\n        mul3_a <= d2; {mul3_b0, mul3_b1, mul3_b2, mul3_b3} <= {C5_16,C5_16,C5_16,C5_16};\n    end\n    3'd4:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= d0; {mul0_b0, mul0_b1, mul0_b2, mul0_b3} <= {C7_16,C7_16,C7_16,C7_16};\n        mul1_a <= d6; {mul1_b0, mul1_b1, mul1_b2, mul1_b3} <= {C1_16,C1_16,C1_16,C1_16};\n        mul2_a <= d4; {mul2_b0, mul2_b1, mul2_b2, mul2_b3} <= {C3_16,C3_16,C3_16,C3_16};\n        mul3_a <= d2; {mul3_b0, mul3_b1, mul3_b2, mul3_b3} <= {C5_16,C5_16,C5_16,C5_16};\n    end\n    3'd5:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= d2; {mul0_b0, mul0_b1, mul0_b2, mul0_b3} <= {C6_16,C6_16,C6_16,C6_16};\n        mul1_a <= d6; {mul1_b0, mul1_b1, mul1_b2, mul1_b3} <= {C2_16,C2_16,C2_16,C2_16};\n        mul4_a <= i0; {mul4_b0, mul4_b1, mul4_b2, mul4_b3} <= {C4_16,C4_16,C4_16,C4_16};\n    end\n    default:\n        ;\n    endcase\n    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */\nend\n\n// 4-way split of each product a*b (exact; |a*b| < 2^28 for all inputs):\n// a = a[3:0] + (a[7:4]<<4) + (a[11:8]<<8) + (a[15:12]<<12)\n// stage 1 registers the four raw partial products (b-fanout = 4);\n// stage 2 combines (carry-save + final CPA).\nwire signed [17:0] q0a = $signed({1'b0, mul0_a[3:0]}) * mul0_b0;\nwire signed [17:0] q0b = $signed(mul0_a[7:4]) * mul0_b1;\nwire signed [17:0] q0c = $signed(mul0_a[11:8]) * mul0_b2;\nwire signed [17:0] q0d = $signed(mul0_a[15:12]) * mul0_b3;\nwire signed [17:0] q1a = $signed({1'b0, mul1_a[3:0]}) * mul1_b0;\nwire signed [17:0] q1b = $signed(mul1_a[7:4]) * mul1_b1;\nwire signed [17:0] q1c = $signed(mul1_a[11:8]) * mul1_b2;\nwire signed [17:0] q1d = $signed(mul1_a[15:12]) * mul1_b3;\nwire signed [17:0] q2a = $signed({1'b0, mul2_a[3:0]}) * mul2_b0;\nwire signed [17:0] q2b = $signed(mul2_a[7:4]) * mul2_b1;\nwire signed [17:0] q2c = $signed(mul2_a[11:8]) * mul2_b2;\nwire signed [17:0] q2d = $signed(mul2_a[15:12]) * mul2_b3;\nwire signed [17:0] q3a = $signed({1'b0, mul3_a[3:0]}) * mul3_b0;\nwire signed [17:0] q3b = $signed(mul3_a[7:4]) * mul3_b1;\nwire signed [17:0] q3c = $signed(mul3_a[11:8]) * mul3_b2;\nwire signed [17:0] q3d = $signed(mul3_a[15:12]) * mul3_b3;\nwire signed [17:0] q4a = $signed({1'b0, mul4_a[3:0]}) * mul4_b0;\nwire signed [17:0] q4b = $signed(mul4_a[7:4]) * mul4_b1;\nwire signed [17:0] q4c = $signed(mul4_a[11:8]) * mul4_b2;\nwire signed [18:0] q4d = $signed(mul4_a[16:12]) * mul4_b3;\n\nreg signed [17:0] A0_q, B0_q, C0_q, D0_q;\nreg signed [17:0] A1_q, B1_q, C1_q, D1_q;\nreg signed [17:0] A2_q, B2_q, C2_q, D2_q;\nreg signed [17:0] A3_q, B3_q, C3_q, D3_q;\nreg signed [17:0] A4_q, B4_q, C4_q;\nreg signed [18:0] D4_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    {A0_q, B0_q, C0_q, D0_q} <= 0;\n    {A1_q, B1_q, C1_q, D1_q} <= 0;\n    {A2_q, B2_q, C2_q, D2_q} <= 0;\n    {A3_q, B3_q, C3_q, D3_q} <= 0;\n    {A4_q, B4_q, C4_q, D4_q} <= 0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    A0_q <= q0a; B0_q <= q0b; C0_q <= q0c; D0_q <= q0d;\n    A1_q <= q1a; B1_q <= q1b; C1_q <= q1c; D1_q <= q1d;\n    A2_q <= q2a; B2_q <= q2b; C2_q <= q2c; D2_q <= q2d;\n    A3_q <= q3a; B3_q <= q3b; C3_q <= q3c; D3_q <= q3d;\n    A4_q <= q4a; B4_q <= q4b; C4_q <= q4c; D4_q <= q4d;\nend\n\n// stage 2: combine 4 shifted partials (exact mod 2^29 == exact)\nwire signed [28:0] w0a = {{11{A0_q[17]}}, A0_q};\nwire signed [28:0] w0b = {{7{B0_q[17]}}, B0_q, 4'b0};\nwire signed [28:0] w0c = {{3{C0_q[17]}}, C0_q, 8'b0};\nwire signed [28:0] w0d = {D0_q, 12'b0};\nwire signed [28:0] w1a = {{11{A1_q[17]}}, A1_q};\nwire signed [28:0] w1b = {{7{B1_q[17]}}, B1_q, 4'b0};\nwire signed [28:0] w1c = {{3{C1_q[17]}}, C1_q, 8'b0};\nwire signed [28:0] w1d = {D1_q, 12'b0};\nwire signed [28:0] w2a = {{11{A2_q[17]}}, A2_q};\nwire signed [28:0] w2b = {{7{B2_q[17]}}, B2_q, 4'b0};\nwire signed [28:0] w2c = {{3{C2_q[17]}}, C2_q, 8'b0};\nwire signed [28:0] w2d = {D2_q, 12'b0};\nwire signed [28:0] w3a = {{11{A3_q[17]}}, A3_q};\nwire signed [28:0] w3b = {{7{B3_q[17]}}, B3_q, 4'b0};\nwire signed [28:0] w3c = {{3{C3_q[17]}}, C3_q, 8'b0};\nwire signed [28:0] w3d = {D3_q, 12'b0};\nwire signed [28:0] w4a = {{11{A4_q[17]}}, A4_q};\nwire signed [28:0] w4b = {{7{B4_q[17]}}, B4_q, 4'b0};\nwire signed [28:0] w4c = {{3{C4_q[17]}}, C4_q, 8'b0};\nwire signed [28:0] w4d = {D4_q, 10'b0};\n\nwire [28:0] u0s = w0a ^ w0b ^ w0c;\nwire [28:0] u0c = (w0a & w0b) | (w0a & w0c) | (w0b & w0c);\nwire [28:0] u0t = u0s ^ (u0c <<< 1) ^ w0d;\nwire [28:0] u0u = (u0s & (u0c <<< 1)) | (u0s & w0d) | ((u0c <<< 1) & w0d);\nwire [28:0] u1s = w1a ^ w1b ^ w1c;\nwire [28:0] u1c = (w1a & w1b) | (w1a & w1c) | (w1b & w1c);\nwire [28:0] u1t = u1s ^ (u1c <<< 1) ^ w1d;\nwire [28:0] u1u = (u1s & (u1c <<< 1)) | (u1s & w1d) | ((u1c <<< 1) & w1d);\nwire [28:0] u2s = w2a ^ w2b ^ w2c;\nwire [28:0] u2c = (w2a & w2b) | (w2a & w2c) | (w2b & w2c);\nwire [28:0] u2t = u2s ^ (u2c <<< 1) ^ w2d;\nwire [28:0] u2u = (u2s & (u2c <<< 1)) | (u2s & w2d) | ((u2c <<< 1) & w2d);\nwire [28:0] u3s = w3a ^ w3b ^ w3c;\nwire [28:0] u3c = (w3a & w3b) | (w3a & w3c) | (w3b & w3c);\nwire [28:0] u3t = u3s ^ (u3c <<< 1) ^ w3d;\nwire [28:0] u3u = (u3s & (u3c <<< 1)) | (u3s & w3d) | ((u3c <<< 1) & w3d);\nwire [28:0] u4s = w4a ^ w4b ^ w4c;\nwire [28:0] u4c = (w4a & w4b) | (w4a & w4c) | (w4b & w4c);\nwire [28:0] u4t = u4s ^ (u4c <<< 1) ^ w4d;\nwire [28:0] u4u = (u4s & (u4c <<< 1)) | (u4s & w4d) | ((u4c <<< 1) & w4d);\n\nreg signed [28:0] mul0, mul1, mul2, mul3, mul4;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    mul0 <= 0; mul1 <= 0; mul2 <= 0; mul3 <= 0; mul4 <= 0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    mul0 <= u0t + (u0u <<< 1);\n    mul1 <= u1t + (u1u <<< 1);\n    mul2 <= u2t + (u2u <<< 1);\n    mul3 <= u3t + (u3u <<< 1);\n    mul4 <= u4t + (u4u <<< 1);\nend\n\nreg        out_stg0_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg0_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg1_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg1_idx_q;\nreg        out_stg2_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg2_idx_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg0_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg1_idx_q <= 3'b0;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg2_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    out_stg0_valid_q <= inport_valid_i; out_stg0_idx_q <= inport_idx_i;\n    out_stg1_valid_q <= out_stg0_valid_q; out_stg1_idx_q <= out_stg0_idx_q;\n    out_stg2_valid_q <= out_stg1_valid_q; out_stg2_idx_q <= out_stg1_idx_q;\nend\n\n// all x-pass internals bounded by 570.7M < 2^30 -> 31-bit signed\nreg signed [30:0] o_s5, o_s6, o_s7;\nreg signed [30:0] o_t0, o_t1, o_t2, o_t3, o_t4, o_t5, o_t6, o_t7;\nreg signed [30:0] o_t6_5, o_t5_6;\n\n//--- 1/sqrt(2) scaling: d = trunc((x*181 mod 2^32)/256) (signed, exact) ------\nwire signed [31:0] px5 = o_t6_5 * 181;\nwire signed [31:0] px6 = o_t5_6 * 181;\nwire        nzx5 = ((o_t6_5[7:0] * 8'd181) != 8'd0);\nwire        nzx6 = ((o_t5_6[7:0] * 8'd181) != 8'd0);\n\nreg signed [23:0] s5hi_q, s6hi_q;   // p >>> 8 (fits 24-bit signed)\nreg signed [31:0] f5_q, f6_q;       // truncation fixup (0 or +1), signed\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    s5hi_q <= 24'b0; s6hi_q <= 24'b0; f5_q <= 32'b0; f6_q <= 32'b0;\nend\nelse if (out_stg2_idx_q == 3'd7)\nbegin\n    s5hi_q <= px5[31:8];\n    s6hi_q <= px6[31:8];\n    f5_q   <= px5[31] & nzx5;\n    f6_q   <= px6[31] & nzx6;\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    o_s5 <= 0; o_s6 <= 0; o_s7 <= 0; o_t0 <= 0; o_t1 <= 0; o_t2 <= 0;\n    o_t3 <= 0; o_t4 <= 0; o_t5 <= 0; o_t6 <= 0; o_t7 <= 0;\n    o_t6_5 <= 0; o_t5_6 <= 0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    case (out_stg2_idx_q)\n    3'd0: begin o_t3 <= mul0 + mul1; end\n    3'd1: begin o_s7 <= mul0 + mul1; o_s6 <= mul2 + mul3; o_t0 <= mul4; end\n    3'd2: begin o_t0 <= o_t0 + o_t3; o_t3 <= o_t0 - o_t3; o_t7 <= o_s6 + o_s7; end\n    3'd3: begin o_t4 <= (mul0 - mul1) + (mul2 - mul3); end\n    3'd4: begin o_t0 <= mul0 - mul1; o_s5 <= mul2 - mul3; end\n    3'd5: begin o_t3 <= mul0 - mul1; o_t4 <= mul4; o_t5 <= o_t0 - o_s5; o_t6 <= o_s7 - o_s6; end\n    3'd6: begin o_t1 <= o_t4 + o_t3; o_t2 <= o_t4 - o_t3; o_t6_5 <= o_t6 - o_t5; o_t5_6 <= o_t5 + o_t6; end\n    default: ;\n    endcase\nend\n\nreg        out_stg3_valid_q;\nreg [2:0]  out_stg3_idx_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= 1'b0; out_stg3_idx_q <= 3'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    out_stg3_valid_q <= out_stg2_valid_q; out_stg3_idx_q <= out_stg2_idx_q;\nend\n\n// |out| <= 346243 < 2^19 -> 20-bit exact\nreg signed [19:0] block_out [0:7];\nreg signed [19:0] block_out_tmp;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_out[0] <= 0; block_out[1] <= 0; block_out[2] <= 0; block_out[3] <= 0;\n    block_out[4] <= 0; block_out[5] <= 0; block_out[6] <= 0; block_out[7] <= 0;\n    block_out_tmp <= 0;\nend\nelse if (out_stg3_valid_q)\nbegin\n    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd3)\n    begin\n        block_out[0] <= ((o_t0 + o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out_tmp <= ((o_t0 - o_t7) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[3] <= ((o_t3 + o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[4] <= ((o_t3 - o_t4) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd6)\n        block_out[7] <= block_out_tmp;\n\n    if (out_stg3_idx_q == 3'd7)\n    begin\n        block_out[2] <= ((o_t2 + s5hi_q + f5_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[5] <= ((o_t2 - s5hi_q - f5_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[1] <= ((o_t1 + s6hi_q + f6_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n        block_out[6] <= ((o_t1 - s6hi_q - f6_q) >>> OUT_SHIFT);\n    end\n    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */\nend\n\nreg [7:0] valid_q;\nreg [5:0] ptr_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    valid_q <= 8'b0;\nelse\n    valid_q <= {valid_q[6:0], out_stg3_valid_q};\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    ptr_q <= 6'd0;\nelse if (outport_valid_o)\n    ptr_q <= ptr_q + 6'd1;\n\nassign outport_valid_o = valid_q[6];\nassign outport_data_o  = block_out[ptr_q[2:0]];\nassign outport_idx_o   = ptr_q;\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// Double-buffered corner turn (4 lanes x 2 halves x 16 entries)\n// Read path: one-hot decode + AND-OR tree (low fanout, shallow).\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jt_ram\n#(\n     parameter W = 20\n)\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           wr_en\n    ,input           block_wr\n    ,input  [  3:0]  wr_addr\n    ,input  [W-1:0]  wr_data\n    ,input           block_rd\n    ,input  [  3:0]  rd_addr\n    ,output reg [W-1:0]  rd_data\n);\n\nreg [W-1:0] mem0 [0:15];\nreg [W-1:0] mem1 [0:15];\n\n// one-hot decode of the read address\nreg [15:0] oh;\ninteger i;\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    for (i = 0; i < 16; i = i + 1)\n        oh[i] = (rd_addr == i);\nend\n\nwire [15:0] oh0 = oh & {16{~block_rd}};\nwire [15:0] oh1 = oh & {16{ block_rd}};\n\n// gated words: exactly one of the 32 is (possibly) non-zero\nreg [W-1:0] gt [0:31];\nreg [W-1:0] t1 [0:15];\nreg [W-1:0] t2 [0:7];\nreg [W-1:0] t3 [0:3];\nreg [W-1:0] t4 [0:1];\nreg [W-1:0] acc;\ninteger k;\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1)\n    begin\n        gt[k]    = mem0[k] & {W{oh0[k]}};\n        gt[k+16] = mem1[k] & {W{oh1[k]}};\n    end\n    for (k = 0; k < 16; k = k + 1) t1[k] = gt[2*k] | gt[2*k+1];\n    for (k = 0; k < 8;  k = k + 1) t2[k] = t1[2*k] | t1[2*k+1];\n    for (k = 0; k < 4;  k = k + 1) t3[k] = t2[2*k] | t2[2*k+1];\n    for (k = 0; k < 2;  k = k + 1) t4[k] = t3[2*k] | t3[2*k+1];\n    acc = t4[0] | t4[1];\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nbegin\n    if (wr_en)\n    begin\n        if (block_wr)\n            mem1[wr_addr] <= wr_data;\n        else\n            mem0[wr_addr] <= wr_data;\n    end\n\n    rd_data <= acc;\nend\n\nendmodule\n\nmodule jpeg_idct_transpose\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data_i\n    ,input  [  5:0]  inport_idx_i\n    ,input           outport_ready_i\n    ,output          inport_accept_o\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data0_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data1_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data2_o\n    ,output [ 19:0]  outport_data3_o\n    ,output [  2:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\nreg         block_wr_q;\nreg         block_rd_q;\nreg [5:0]   rd_idx_q;\nreg [3:0]   rd_addr_q;\nreg [3:0]   rda0_q, rda1_q, rda2_q, rda3_q;\n\nwire [3:0]  wr_addr_w = inport_idx_i[3:0];\n\nwire wr0_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd0);\nwire wr1_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd1);\nwire wr2_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd2);\nwire wr3_w = inport_valid_i && inport_accept_o && (inport_idx_i[5:4] == 2'd3);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram0\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr0_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda0_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data0_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram1\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr1_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda1_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data1_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram2\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr2_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda2_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data2_o)\n);\n\njt_ram\n#(.W(20))\nu_ram3\n(\n     .clk(clk)\n    ,.wr_en(wr3_w)\n    ,.block_wr(block_wr_q)\n    ,.wr_addr(wr_addr_w)\n    ,.wr_data(inport_data_i)\n    ,.block_rd(block_rd_q)\n    ,.rd_addr(rda3_q)\n    ,.rd_data(outport_data3_o)\n);\n\nreg [1:0] block_ready_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q    <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q    <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse if (img_start_i)\nbegin\n    block_ready_q <= 2'b0;\n    block_wr_q    <= 1'b0;\n    block_rd_q    <= 1'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    if (inport_valid_i && inport_idx_i == 6'd63 && inport_accept_o)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q[block_wr_q] <= 1'b1;\n        block_wr_q                <= ~block_wr_q;\n    end\n\n    if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q[5:0] == 6'd63)\n    begin\n        block_ready_q[block_rd_q] <= 1'b0;\n        block_rd_q <= ~block_rd_q;\n    end\nend\n\nassign inport_accept_o = ~block_ready_q[block_wr_q];\n\nlocalparam STATE_W           = 2;\nlocalparam STATE_IDLE        = 2'd0;\nlocalparam STATE_SETUP       = 2'd1;\nlocalparam STATE_ACTIVE      = 2'd2;\n\nreg [STATE_W-1:0] state_q;\nreg [STATE_W-1:0] next_state_r;\n\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    next_state_r = state_q;\n    case (state_q)\n    STATE_IDLE:\n        if (block_ready_q[block_rd_q] && outport_ready_i)\n            next_state_r = STATE_SETUP;\n    STATE_SETUP:\n        next_state_r = STATE_ACTIVE;\n    STATE_ACTIVE:\n        if (outport_valid_o && rd_idx_q == 6'd63)\n            next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\n    default: ;\n    endcase\n    if (img_start_i)\n        next_state_r = STATE_IDLE;\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    state_q <= STATE_IDLE;\nelse\n    state_q <= next_state_r;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\n    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;\nelse if (img_start_i)\n    rd_idx_q <= 6'b0;\nelse if (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE)\n    rd_idx_q <= rd_idx_q + 6'd1;\n\n// read address (identical schedule to original), replicated per RAM to\n// cut select-net fanout\nreg [3:0] rda_next;\n\nalways @ *\nbegin\n    case (state_q)\n    STATE_IDLE:\n        rda_next = 4'd0;\n    STATE_SETUP:\n        rda_next = 4'd8;\n    STATE_ACTIVE:\n    begin\n        case (rd_idx_q[2:0])\n        3'd0: rda_next = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd1: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        3'd2: rda_next = rd_addr_q;\n        3'd3: rda_next = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd4: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        3'd5: rda_next = rd_addr_q - 4'd8;\n        3'd6: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd1;\n        3'd7: rda_next = rd_addr_q + 4'd8;\n        endcase\n    end\n    default:\n        rda_next = rd_addr_q;\n    endcase\nend\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    rd_addr_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda0_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda1_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda2_q <= 4'b0;\n    rda3_q <= 4'b0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    rd_addr_q <= rda_next;\n    rda0_q <= rda_next;\n    rda1_q <= rda_next;\n    rda2_q <= rda_next;\n    rda3_q <= rda_next;\nend\n\nassign outport_valid_o = (state_q == STATE_ACTIVE);\nassign outport_idx_o   = rd_idx_q[2:0];\n\nendmodule\n\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\n// 1-D IDCT, column pass (inputs are the 20-bit x-pass results)\n// products exact mod 2^32 (identical to the original 32-bit multiply)\n//-----------------------------------------------------------------\nmodule jpeg_idct_y\n(\n     input           clk\n    ,input           rst\n    ,input           img_start_i\n    ,input           img_end_i\n    ,input           inport_valid_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data0_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data1_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data2_i\n    ,input  [ 19:0]  inport_data3_i\n    ,input  [  2:0]  inport_idx_i\n    ,output          outport_valid_o\n    ,output [ 31:0]  outport_data_o\n    ,output [  5:0]  outport_idx_o\n);\n\nlocalparam OUT_SHIFT = 15;\n\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C1_16 = 13'sd4017;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C2_16 = 13'sd3784;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C3_16 = 13'sd3406;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C4_16 = 13'sd2896;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C5_16 = 13'sd2276;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C6_16 = 13'sd1567;\nlocalparam signed [12:0] C7_16 = 13'sd799;\n\nwire signed [19:0] block_in_0_1 = inport_data0_i;\nwire signed [19:0] block_in_2_3 = inport_data1_i;\nwire signed [19:0] block_in_4_5 = inport_data2_i;\nwire signed [19:0] block_in_6_7 = inport_data3_i;\n\nreg signed [20:0] i0;\nreg signed [19:0] mul0_a, mul1_a, mul2_a, mul3_a;\nreg signed [20:0] mul4_a;\nreg signed [12:0] mul0_b0, mul0_b1, mul0_b2, mul0_b3;\nreg signed [12:0] mul1_b0, mul1_b1, mul1_b2, mul1_b3;\nreg signed [12:0] mul2_b0, mul2_b1, mul2_b2, mul2_b3;\nreg signed [12:0] mul3_b0, mul3_b1, mul3_b2, mul3_b3;\nreg signed [12:0] mul4_b0, mul4_b1, mul4_b2, mul4_b3;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    i0 <= 21'b0;\n    mul0_a <= 20'b0; {mul0_b0, mul0_b1, mul0_b2, mul0_b3} <= 0;\n    mul1_a <= 20'b0; {mul1_b0, mul1_b1, mul1_b2, mul1_b3} <= 0;\n    mul2_a <= 20'b0; {mul2_b0, mul2_b1, mul2_b2, mul2_b3} <= 0;\n    mul3_a <= 20'b0; {mul3_b0, mul3_b1, mul3_b2, mul3_b3} <= 0;\n    mul4_a <= 21'b0; {mul4_b0, mul4_b1, mul4_b2, mul4_b3} <= 0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    /* verilator lint_off WIDTH */\n    case (inport_idx_i)\n    3'd0:\n    begin\n        i0     <= block_in_0_1 + block_in_4_5;\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; {mul0_b0, mul0_b1, mul0_b2, mul0_b3} <= {C2_16,C2_16,C2_16,C2_16};\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; {mul1_b0, mul1_b1, mul1_b2, mul1_b3} <= {C6_16,C6_16,C6_16,C6_16};\n    end\n    3'd1:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; {mul0_b0, mul0_b1, mul0_b2, mul0_b3} <= {C1_16,C1_16,C1_16,C1_16};\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; {mul1_b0, mul1_b1, mul1_b2, mul1_b3} <= {C7_16,C7_16,C7_16,C7_16};\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; {mul2_b0, mul2_b1, mul2_b2, mul2_b3} <= {C5_16,C5_16,C5_16,C5_16};\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; {mul3_b0, mul3_b1, mul3_b2, mul3_b3} <= {C3_16,C3_16,C3_16,C3_16};\n        mul4_a <= i0;           {mul4_b0, mul4_b1, mul4_b2, mul4_b3} <= {C4_16,C4_16,C4_16,C4_16};\n    end\n    3'd2:\n    begin\n        i0     <= block_in_0_1 - block_in_4_5;\n    end\n    3'd3:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; {mul0_b0, mul0_b1, mul0_b2, mul0_b3} <= {C7_16,C7_16,C7_16,C7_16};\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; {mul1_b0, mul1_b1, mul1_b2, mul1_b3} <= {C1_16,C1_16,C1_16,C1_16};\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; {mul2_b0, mul2_b1, mul2_b2, mul2_b3} <= {C3_16,C3_16,C3_16,C3_16};\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; {mul3_b0, mul3_b1, mul3_b2, mul3_b3} <= {C5_16,C5_16,C5_16,C5_16};\n    end\n    3'd4:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_0_1; {mul0_b0, mul0_b1, mul0_b2, mul0_b3} <= {C7_16,C7_16,C7_16,C7_16};\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; {mul1_b0, mul1_b1, mul1_b2, mul1_b3} <= {C1_16,C1_16,C1_16,C1_16};\n        mul2_a <= block_in_4_5; {mul2_b0, mul2_b1, mul2_b2, mul2_b3} <= {C3_16,C3_16,C3_16,C3_16};\n        mul3_a <= block_in_2_3; {mul3_b0, mul3_b1, mul3_b2, mul3_b3} <= {C5_16,C5_16,C5_16,C5_16};\n    end\n    3'd5:\n    begin\n        mul0_a <= block_in_2_3; {mul0_b0, mul0_b1, mul0_b2, mul0_b3} <= {C6_16,C6_16,C6_16,C6_16};\n        mul1_a <= block_in_6_7; {mul1_b0, mul1_b1, mul1_b2, mul1_b3} <= {C2_16,C2_16,C2_16,C2_16};\n        mul4_a <= i0;           {mul4_b0, mul4_b1, mul4_b2, mul4_b3} <= {C4_16,C4_16,C4_16,C4_16};\n    end\n    default:\n        ;\n    endcase\n    /* verilator lint_on WIDTH */\nend\n\n// 4-way split of each product a*b, exact mod 2^32 (as the original):\n// a = a[4:0] + (a[9:5]<<5) + (a[14:10]<<10) + (a[19:15]<<15)\n// stage 1 registers the four raw partial products (b-fanout = 5);\n// stage 2 combines (carry-save + final CPA).\nwire signed [18:0] q0a = $signed({1'b0, mul0_a[4:0]}) * mul0_b0;\nwire signed [18:0] q0b = $signed(mul0_a[9:5]) * mul0_b1;\nwire signed [18:0] q0c = $signed(mul0_a[14:10]) * mul0_b2;\nwire signed [18:0] q0d = $signed(mul0_a[19:15]) * mul0_b3;\nwire signed [18:0] q1a = $signed({1'b0, mul1_a[4:0]}) * mul1_b0;\nwire signed [18:0] q1b = $signed(mul1_a[9:5]) * mul1_b1;\nwire signed [18:0] q1c = $signed(mul1_a[14:10]) * mul1_b2;\nwire signed [18:0] q1d = $signed(mul1_a[19:15]) * mul1_b3;\nwire signed [18:0] q2a = $signed({1'b0, mul2_a[4:0]}) * mul2_b0;\nwire signed [18:0] q2b = $signed(mul2_a[9:5]) * mul2_b1;\nwire signed [18:0] q2c = $signed(mul2_a[14:10]) * mul2_b2;\nwire signed [18:0] q2d = $signed(mul2_a[19:15]) * mul2_b3;\nwire signed [18:0] q3a = $signed({1'b0, mul3_a[4:0]}) * mul3_b0;\nwire signed [18:0] q3b = $signed(mul3_a[9:5]) * mul3_b1;\nwire signed [18:0] q3c = $signed(mul3_a[14:10]) * mul3_b2;\nwire signed [18:0] q3d = $signed(mul3_a[19:15]) * mul3_b3;\nwire signed [18:0] q4a = $signed({1'b0, mul4_a[4:0]}) * mul4_b0;\nwire signed [18:0] q4b = $signed(mul4_a[9:5]) * mul4_b1;\nwire signed [18:0] q4c = $signed(mul4_a[14:10]) * mul4_b2;\nwire signed [19:0] q4d = $signed(mul4_a[20:15]) * mul4_b3;\n\nreg signed [18:0] A0_q, B0_q, C0_q, D0_q;\nreg signed [18:0] A1_q, B1_q, C1_q, D1_q;\nreg signed [18:0] A2_q, B2_q, C2_q, D2_q;\nreg signed [18:0] A3_q, B3_q, C3_q, D3_q;\nreg signed [18:0] A4_q, B4_q, C4_q;\nreg signed [19:0] D4_q;\n\nalways @ (posedge clk)\nif (rst)\nbegin\n    {A0_q, B0_q, C0_q, D0_q} <= 0;\n    {A1_q, B1_q, C1_q, D1_q} <= 0;\n    {A2_q, B2_q, C2_q, D2_q} <= 0;\n    {A3_q, B3_q, C3_q, D3_q} <= 0;\n    {A4_q, B4_q, C4_q, D4_q} <= 0;\nend\nelse\nbegin\n    A0_q <= q0a; B0_q <= q0b; C0_q <= q0c; D0_q <= q0d;\n    A1_q <= q1a; B1_q <= q1b; C1_q <= q1c; D1_q <= q1d;\n    A2_q <= q2a; B2_q <= q2b; C2_q <= q2c; D2_q <= q2d;\n    A3_q <= q3a; B3_q <= q3b; C3_q <= q3c; D3_q <= q3d;\n    A4_q <= q4a; B4_q <= q4b; C4_q <= q4c; D4_q <= q4d;\nend\n\n// stage 2: combine 4 shifted partials (exact mod 2^32)\nwire signed [31:0] v0a = {{13{A0_q[18]}}, A0_q};\nwire signed [31:0] v0b = {{8{B0_q[18]}}, B0_q, 5'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] v0c = {{3{C0_q[18]}}, C0_q, 10'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] v0d = {D0_q, 12'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] v1a = {{13{A1_q[18]}}, A1_q};\nwire signed [31:0] v1b = {{8{B1_q[18]}}, B1_q, 5'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] v1c = {{3{C1_q[18]}}, C1_q, 10'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] v1d = {D1_q, 12'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] v2a = {{13{A2_q[18]}}, A2_q};\nwire signed [31:0] v2b = {{8{B2_q[18]}}, B2_q, 5'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] v2c = {{3{C2_q[18]}}, C2_q, 10'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] v2d = {D2_q, 12'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] v3a = {{13{A3_q[18]}}, A3_q};\nwire signed [31:0] v3b = {{8{B3_q[18]}}, B3_q, 5'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] v3c = {{3{C3_q[18]}}, C3_q, 10'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] v3d = {D3_q, 12'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] v4a = {{13{A4_q[18]}}, A4_q};\nwire signed [31:0] v4b = {{8{B4_q[18]}}, B4_q, 5'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] v4c = {{3{C4_q[18]}}, C4_q, 10'b0};\nwire signed [31:0] v4d = {D4_q, 11'b0};\n\nwire [31:0] r0s = v0a ^ v0b ^ v0c;\nwire [31:0] r0c = (v0a & v0b) | (v0a & v0c) | (v0b & v0c);\nwire [31:0] r0t = r0s ^ (r0c <<< 1) ^ v0d;\nwire [31:0] r0u = (r0s & (r0c <<< 1)) | (r0s & v0d) | ((r0c <<< 1) & v0d);\nwire [31:0] r1s = v1a ^ v1b ^ v1c;\nwire [31:0] r1c = (v1a & v1b) | (v1a & v1c) | (v1b & v1c);\nwire [31:0] r1t = r1s ^ (r1c <<< 1) ^ v1d;\nwire [31:0] r1u = (r1s & (r1c <<< 1)) | (r1s & v1d) | ((r1c <<< 1) & v1d);\nwire [31:0] r2s = v2a ^ v2b ^ v2c;\nwire [31:0] r2c = (v2a & v2b) | (v2a & v2c) | (v2b & v2c);\nwire [31:0] r2t = r2s ^ (r2c <<< 1) ^ v2d;\nwire [31:0] r2u = (r2s & (r2c <<< 1)) | (r2s & v2d) | ((r2c <<< 1) & v2d);\nwire [31:0] r3s = v3a ^ v3b ^ v3c;\nwire [31:0] r3c = (v3a & v3b) | (v3a & v3c) | (v3b & v3c);\nwire [31:0] r3t = r3s ## Session Summary

**Best result: `design_v8.v` — PASS, cost 4.163e6 area_delay_product** (13.05e6 for the original starting point → **3.1× improvement**; delay 3053→1004 ps, area 4274→4146).

### 1. Approaches tried (in order)
- **Baseline copy** (`design.v`): original core_jpeg composition verbatim — 13.05e6.
- **v2** (`design_v2.v`): removed unused transpose RAM read port, 2-level read mux, narrowed multipliers — *slower* (mux got worse).
- **v3** (`design_v3.v`): **one-hot AND-OR transpose read path** + exact signed-division rewrite — 5.75e6. Big win.
- **v4** (`design_v4.v`): exact `(x*181)/256` via early low-byte zero-detect (181 is odd ⇒ `(x·181) mod 256 == 0 ⇔ x[7:0]==0`) + fixup folded into the downstream output add — **4.33e6**.
- **v6/v7/v8**: multiplier restructuring into 2-stage chunked partial products; **v8 (7-bit/7-bit/top 3-way split, per-array b-register copies) won at 4.163e6**.
- **v9/v10/v11**: carry-save (3:2) combine registered instead of raw partials; consumer-side recombination; 4-way splits — all failed or regressed.

### 2. Highest-impact optimizations
1. **Transpose read mux → one-hot decode + balanced AND-OR tree** (v3): 3446→1437 ps. The 32:1 mux over 4×20-bit RAM arrays was the single worst structure.
2. **Replacing the signed `/256` divider** with early operand-based zero-detect + fixup folded into an existing adder (v4): 1437→1057 ps, and removed two 32-bit dividers + increment adders.
3. **Proven width minimization** (mathematical range analysis on the fixed algorithm): x-pass outputs 20-bit, y-pass outputs 17-bit, constants 13-bit, products exact at 28–32 bits — allowed narrow multipliers everywhere without any behavior change.
4. **Multiplier chunking** (v8): 7b+7b+top 3-way split with duplicated b-registers (fanout control) → 1004 ps.

### 3. What didn't work / regressed
- **v10** (carry-save pair registered, consumers recombine): broke pipeline alignment — consumers are scheduled one cycle after the register, so the recombination must be *in* the registered stage. 19k errors.
- **v11** (4-way split): three separate bugs — middle chunks of a split operand must be **zero-extended** (only the top chunk is signed); wrong shift constants for the top chunks (10/12 instead of 12/15). Still failing at session end (12k errors) — likely one more residual sign/shift slip; abandoned rather than debugged further.
- **`target_delay` sweeps** (250/500/1200 ps): netlist essentially identical — ABC area-maps when the constraint is unreachable and doesn't relax when it's loose. RTL structure is the only real lever in this flow.
- Naive "fold fixup into the multiply" (v3 first attempt): inexact for |x·181| ≥ 2³¹ (product sign-bit flips) — 3 vector misses; had to use the exact product form.

### 4. Lessons learned
- **Verilog signedness is the #1 bug source**: a 1-bit unsigned term poisons an entire expression's signedness (turning `>>>` into logical shift — v4's 11k errors). Declare even 0/+1 fixup values as `signed [31:0]`.
- In a multi-chunk partial-product split: **only the top chunk is signed; every middle chunk must be zero-extended**, and each chunk's shift must equal its bit offset — verify by expanding `a = Σ chunkᵢ·2^(kᵢ)` on paper before writing RTL.
- Check **register-load vs. consume timing** explicitly when restructuring pipelines (the v4 off-by-one and v10 one-cycle-early bugs were both silent structural slips, not math errors).
- Range proofs (all-input bounds, not just testvector-observed ranges) are extremely profitable here: JPEG IDCT has tight analytic bounds, so 20/17-bit storage and narrow multipliers are *exact*, not approximate.
- Don't waste steps on synthesis-knob sweeps when the tool provably ignores them; spend them on datapath structure.
- Errors-in-unexpected-places usually means a structural/scheduling bug, not an arithmetic one — inspect the code before theorizing about overflow.
